2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 美國聯準會如市場預期 ，維持政策利率不變。 決策官員仍對通膨保持擔憂 ， 也承
認美國經濟正以「強勁速度」成長。聯準會主席Kevin Warsh在會後談話中，並
未明確排除進一步緊縮政策的可能，激勵市場對12 月升息的預期升溫。數據方
面，美國第三季 GDP 年化成長率達 3.4%，高於前值的 2.2%，亦優於市場預期
的 3.2%，印證美國經濟動能強勁。此外，9 月 PCE 與核心 PCE 符合預期，但凸
顯通膨壓力持續偏高。在 「通膨高企」 與 「經濟強韌」 雙重因素驅動下，市場預
期聯準會將於 12 月升息，為美元指數提供有力支撐。惟近期受 AI 產業股票波
動加劇影響，部分資金轉入美債避險，導致美元指數在高檔面臨調節性賣壓。
⚫ 儘管受能源價格走揚影響，9 月歐元區通膨率升至 3.8%，但在薪資壓力維持溫
和，且歐洲央行尚未觀察到第二輪通膨效應全面擴散的情況下 ，歐洲央行上週決
議維持政策利率不變 。通膨回升令市場預期歐洲央行將在下次會議升息 ， 為歐元

提供部分支撐 ， 但考量歐元區經濟成長表現仍落後美國 ， 在美元基本面強勁的抑
制下 ， 歐元反彈力道相對有限 。 目前歐元最大的潛在風險在於部分國家的債券市
場壓力尚未消散，市場對歐洲財政風險仍保持高度警戒。
⚫ 日本央行維持政策利率於 1.25%不變，並且首次明確表示已達成其 2%的通膨目
標；過去日本央行雖認為通膨改善 ， 但始終強調尚未穩定達標，如今首次正式承
認達成 2%目標，代表未來升息門檻進一步下降。此外，日本央行將經濟成長預
測維持在 0.7%至 0.8%，並小幅上修 CPI 預測，令市場預期日央 12 月有望再次
升息，日本貨幣政策正常化將有助日圓結束疲軟走勢。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Nov/2/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週台幣匯價高點為 32.390，低點在 32.676，以 32.478 作收，較前一周收盤價
升值 3 分。 
上週台幣先升後貶，周初，市場等待聯準會利率決議，交投偏謹慎觀望，而外
資在前周大幅匯出之後，轉為雙向進出，台幣止跌回穩，最高來到 32.390 的周內高
點；然而周間，美國科技股財報表現失色，台股賣壓沉重，外資賣超台股並再度轉
為大幅匯出，台幣走貶，最低來到 32.676，不過匯價逢高吸引出口商賣壓出籠，加
上央行提供流動性，台幣貶幅收斂，終場在央行調節之下收在 32.478。

＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣偏弱震盪。就國際市場來看，聯準會 10 月利率決策維持利率不
變，但有兩名官員主張升息 1 碼，且聯準會表示通膨仍處於高檔，顯示聯準會對通
膨仍保持警戒，市場預期美國利率將維持高檔，台美利差仍維持在相對高位，不利
台幣表現；另一方面，美國科技股財報表現不如預期，引發市場對AI 投資回報的疑
慮，對台股也帶來壓力。就台灣市場來看，外資持續調節台股並大幅匯出，10 月累
計賣超將近兆元，資金匯出壓力大，不過一方面央行持續進場調節，加上匯價逢高
預計吸引出口商進場拋匯，料將限制台幣貶值速度。綜合以上，預估本週台幣走勢
偏弱。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：32.300-32.800

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Nov/2/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣持續走升。周初市場等待美國聯準會利率決議，交投偏向觀望，境
外人民幣主要於 6.6600 上方震盪； 周間聯準會如預期維持利率不變 ，美元指數自 102
上方回落 ， 加上中國出口結匯需求持續提供支撐， 境外人民幣突破6.6600 整數關口；
週五人行中間價續創近三年九個月新高，最高來到 6.7138，顯示中國人行允許人民
幣溫和升值的走勢，境外人民幣一度升破 6.6500 整數關口，最高來到 6.6487，終場
收在 6.6528，週線升值 162 點，境內人民幣則收在 6.6558，週線升值 146 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣偏升 。 就中國國內市場來看 ，10 月官方製造業 PMI 降至 49.3，
重新落入景氣收縮區間，反映中國內需及製造業動能仍有待改善，不過 9 月出口年
增 36.3%，出口商結匯需求持續提供人民幣支撐，加上人行中間價續創近三年九個
月新高，預計將持續帶動人民幣走升；就國際市場來看，聯準會 10 月利率決策維持
利率不變，但有兩名官員主張升息 1 碼，顯示聯準會對通膨仍保持警戒，市場預期
美國利率將維持高檔 ， 美中利差維持在相對高位 ，將限制人民幣升值的速度與幅度 ；
最後就技術面來看 ，USD/CNH 日線圖中，各天期均線向下發散 ，匯價維持在五日均
線之下交投，均線呈現空頭排列，顯示空方勢力較盛，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣
偏升。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6200-6.6700

### Page 5

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Nov/02/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY 上漲。週初，美伊衝突仍陷僵局支撐美元避險買盤，USD/JPY 最
高來到 158.34；週間，聯準會公布利率維持利率不變，USD/JPY 持續在 158 附近盤
整；臨近週末，日本央行公布利率維持不變並釋放偏鷹訊號， USD/JPY 最低來到
156.94，終場收在 157.53，週線上漲 72 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本央行於上周五宣布利
率維持在 1.25%不變，並表示隨著基礎通膨率大致達到 2%，央行必須持續控制通
膨穩定於此目標，且再次強調貨幣政策已進入更為緊縮階段，未來每隔數月即可能
升息。此外，日本央行在季度報告中，亦將截至 2028 年 3 月財年的核心消費者通
膨預估自三個月前預測的 2.4%上調至 2.6%。日本央行偏鷹立場令市場預期日本最
快將於 12 月再次升息，預計將有利日圓表現。二、聯準會宣布維持利率於 3.75%-
4%區間不變，惟因通膨仍處高位，聯準會重申將致力實現 2%的長期通膨區間， 
FedWatch 顯示 12 月升息機率約七成，美元持穩，預計將限縮 USD/JPY 下行空
間。三、就技術面而言，在日線圖中，USD/JPY 各天期均線趨平，近期匯價大致
在 156 至 158 間震盪，匯價暫無明確方向性。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY 本週呈現
區間震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日圓：156.00-158.50 
前週交易區間 
156.63 158.34

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Nov/02/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，美伊衝突持續以及法國政經情勢動盪等情況下，
歐元走跌，最低來到 1.1111；週間，聯準會和歐洲央行相繼公布利率決議均維持不
變，歐洲央行發表言論偏鷹派，歐元止跌回升至 1.1214；臨近週末，歐元區公布經
濟數據好壞參半，歐元自高點回落，終場收在 1.1158，週線下跌 41 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、歐洲央行如市場預期宣布
存款利率維持在 2.5%不變，並警告能源成本持續上漲將對通膨造成上行風險，暗示
可能在 12 月再次升息 ， 升息預期升溫預計將支撐歐元走勢 。二 、 歐元區第三季GDP
年增 1.3%， 高於市場預期的1.1%， 顯示在能源價格持續上漲的不利因素下 ，經濟仍
展現韌性，預計有利歐元表現。三、聯準會宣布維持利率在 3.75%-4%不變，並重申
致力控制通膨的立場 。 此外 ，美國公布第三季 GDP 成長 3.4%， 優於市場預期的3.2%，
9 月核心 PCE 年增率自前月的 3%上升至 3.1%，在美國經濟強勁及通膨仍高的情況
下 ， 市場預期聯準會將於 12 月升息一碼，美元持穩 ，預計將限縮歐元走升空間 。四、
技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 各天期均線向下發散，不過近期匯價在多次向
下測試 1.1120 附近均未有效跌破 ， 顯示下方仍有買盤支撐。綜合以上 ， 預估EUR/USD
本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1050-1.1270 
前週交易區間 
1.1103 1.1226

7  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115.10.27-115.10.30 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.830%  
30 天期短票利率   1.66% ~ 1.68% 
公債－10 年期-A15109R 2.03% 
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.845%~1.846%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分 ， 時序邁入月底 ， 銀行間已大致調整好積數 ， 且央行未明顯
增發定存單，令市場資金充裕。近兩日股市回落，令週五資金需求上揚，惟供
需尚屬平衡，因此短率持穩於近期區間。 
週 五 隔 夜 拆 款 利 率 區 間 在 0.805%~0.825% ， 30 天 期 短 票 主 要 成 交 在
1.66%~1.68%，利率大致持平於上週。 
二、 本週台債市場交投依舊清淡 ，10 年期指標券 A15109R 等殖系統成交在 2.03%，
5 年期指標券 A15108 議價成交在 1.845%~1.846%。 
美債利率自波段高點回落，且央行總裁楊金龍本週立法院發言維持近來對通
膨容忍度變寬之態度 ， 惟市場對央行升息預期並未完全消除 ， 買盤追價動力有
限，預期台債殖利率維持區間整理，關注下週30 年期公債標售果，預估 5 年
券區間為 1.80%~1.85%，10 年券區間為 1.99%~2.04%。

2026.11.04 
陳靖元 C00190@ibfs.com.tw 
2344.TT 華邦電 
 持續看好營運強勁成長

[法說會更新]   
   公司簡介 
華邦電子是利基型記憶體與積體電路解決方案供應商，核心產品包括 DRAM、NOR 
Flash、NAND Flash 以及邏輯 IC 產品，廣泛應用於手持裝置、消費性電子、電腦周
邊 、 車用與工業電子等市場 。2026 年產品組合為 CMS 59%、Flash31%、Logic 10%。    
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
強力買進 205 
【前次投 資建議 】強力 買進，240 
【大盤指數/股價】43308 / 146

➢ 3Q26 報價漲幅強勁，惟位元出貨量下滑 
3Q26 營收 823.3 億元，QoQ+37.58%，YoY+278.18%，成長動能主要
仍來自於記憶體短缺所帶來的報價強勁漲幅，惟公司為因應 LTA 需求而
實行嚴格控貨策略，導致出貨量呈現下滑。獲利表現方面，3Q26 毛利率
約 70.57%，QoQ+4.32ppts，YoY+23.88ppts， 稅後淨利354.22 億元 ，
QoQ+45.67%，YoY+1,103.54%，EPS 為 7.58 元。  
➢ 4Q26 Flash 產品仍具強勁上漲動能，獲利表現可望持續提升 
展望 4Q26，預估 DRAM 產品 ASP 漲幅約逾 10%，NOR 產品漲幅可望
逾 20%。 預估4Q26 營收約 994.8 億元 ，QoQ+20.83%，YoY+273.64%，
毛利率 72.25%， 稅後淨利416 億元 ，QoQ+17.44%，YoY+1,115.55%，
EPS 為 8.81 元。  
➢ 2027 年 DRAM 及 Flash 新產能開出，挹注營收成長動能 
公司目標將高雄廠產能擴增至 24K，隨新產能於 2H26 投片，且新增產
能皆將採用 16nm 製程，逐步取代舊有的 25Snm 與部分 20nm 製程，
位元出貨量預估將會大幅增加。考量記憶體供需吃緊帶動報價漲幅優於
預期 ， 且隨著位元出貨量逐季成長 ， 投研部預估2026 年全年營收約 2799
億元，毛利率 67.89%，稅後淨利 1114.5 億元，EPS 為 24.04 元。展望
2027 年，預期全球市場將面臨更為緊俏且吃緊的供需格局，而受惠於約
5K 的產能擴充，Flash 2027 年的位元出貨量成長率可望突破 40%。
DRAM 方面 ， 高雄廠則預計滿載月產能將達24~25K， 並透過製程轉換 ，
提高 16nm 製程比重，進一步推成位元出貨量。投研部預估 2027 年營
收約 6,008 億 元 ， 毛 利 率71.21% ， 稅 後 淨 利 為2,550 億元，
YoY+128.84%，EPS 為 54.03 元。  
➢ CUBE 及矽電容陸續貢獻營收，中長期營運動能正向 
評價方面，考量公司 3Q26 ASP 漲勢優於先前預期，且 4Q26 Flash 產
品仍具上漲空間，再加上 2027 年位元出貨量可望迎來顯著成長，營收
成長動能強勁之餘，中長期亦有 CUBE 以及矽電容營收貢獻，預期營運
至 2028 年可望維持高成長態勢，故投研部維持強力買進建議，目標價
205 元(2027F PE 4X)。

2344 華邦電 加權指數

個股報告

公司簡介 
華邦電子股份有限公司成立於 1987 年 9 月，公司在中科與南科高雄園區各設有一座
12 吋晶圓廠 。華邦電子是利基型記憶體與積體電路解決方案供應商 ， 同時擁有自有產
品及技術，從產品設計、研發、晶圓製造到品牌行銷完整垂直整合。核心產品包括
DRAM、NOR Flash、NAND Flash 以及邏輯 IC 產品，廣泛應用於手持裝置、消費性
電子 、 電腦周邊 、 車用與工業電子等市場 。2026 年產品組合為 CMS53%、Flash 35%、
Logic 11%。 
圖 1：華邦電產品組合

資料來源：公司、國票投顧預估 
3Q26 報價漲幅強勁，惟位元出貨量下滑 
3Q26 營收 823.3 億元，QoQ+37.58%，YoY+278.18%，營收成長動能主要仍來自
於記憶體短缺所帶來的報價強勁漲幅 ，惟營收仍低於先前預期 ， 主要因公司為因應 LTA
需求而策略性保留部分產能，實行嚴格控貨策略，導致產能利用率雖然滿載，出貨量
呈現下滑。產品報價漲幅方面皆優於預期，DRAM 產品 ASP 約季增 60%，Flash 產
品 ASP 則季增逾 30%；然而在位元出貨量方面，DRAM 出貨量約季減 8%，Flash 產
品則受惠於高容量 SLC/NOR 需求維持強勁，產能利用率滿載，出貨量與 2Q26 大致
持平。獲利表現方面，3Q26 毛利率約 70.57%，QoQ+4.32ppts，YoY+23.88ppts，
稅後淨利 354.22 億元，QoQ+45.67%，YoY+1,103.54%，EPS 為 7.58 元。 
4Q26 Flash 產品仍具強勁上漲動能，獲利表現可望持續提升 
展望 4Q26，雖然面臨消費性電子產品淡季修正，部分客戶在 4Q26 進入庫存調節，
然而 AI 伺服器、車用與 Edge AI 等領域的拉貨動能依然保持強勁。預估 DRAM 產品
ASP 漲幅約逾 10%，NOR 產品則受惠於 AI 伺服器對 512Mb 以上高容量 NOR Flash
的大量需求，短期缺口難以填補，市場報價仍具備繼續上行的空間，預估 ASP 漲幅仍
可望逾 20%。位元出貨量方面，預期 DRAM 可望受惠製程升級以及良率提升，出貨
量下滑幅度可望收斂，Flash 產品則可望在台中廠產能將針對 24 奈米進行小量擴產，
帶動位元出 貨量逐季 提升。預估 4Q26 營收約 994.8 億 元，QoQ+20.83% ，
YoY+273.64%，毛利率 72.25%，QoQ+1.68ppts，YoY+30.38ppts，稅後淨利 416
億元，QoQ+17.44%，YoY+1,115.55%，EPS 為 8.81 元。

個股報告 
2027 年 DRAM 及 Flash 新產能開出，挹注營收成長動能 
目前高雄廠月產能約為 15K，公司目標在年底前將 16nm 機台買足至 16K 的投片規
模，並計畫在年底前將高雄廠產能擴增至 24K，隨著新產能於 2H26 開始投片，且新
增產能皆將採用 16nm 製程，逐步取代舊有的 25Snm 與部分 20nm 製程，位元出貨
量預估將會大幅增加，預估 2H26 僅少量 16nm 產品將貢獻營收，預計 2027 年營收
貢獻將大幅提升。考量記憶體供需吃緊帶動報價漲幅優於預期，且隨著位元出貨量逐
季成長，投研部預估 2026 年全年營收約 2,799 億元，YoY+213%，毛利率 67.89%，
稅後淨利 1,114.5 億元，YoY+2713.11%，EPS 為 24.04 元。 
展望 2027 年，預期全球市場將面臨更為緊俏且吃緊的供需格局，而受惠於約 5K 的
產能擴充，Flash 2027 年的位元出貨量成長率可望突破 40%。此外，由於原廠陸續將
低容量 2D NAND EOL，促使低容量 eMMC 需求轉向 SLC NAND，使 SLC NAND 的
供給在 2027 年維持緊縮狀態。此外，在伺服器端，預估全新的AI 伺服器機櫃，單一
機櫃將採用達 500 顆至 600 顆的 NOR 與 NAND Flash 晶片，尤其拉動 512Mb、
1Gb 及 2Gb 等高容量快閃記憶體的出貨。DRAM 方面，預計 2027 年將增加來自於
Enterprise SSD 的營收貢獻，目前華邦電已經在進行送樣，看好 Enterprise SSD 需
求持續提升之下，可望為華邦電帶來額外 10%的 DRAM 營收貢獻。產能方面，如前
段所述，預計台中廠將額外擴增 5K Flash 產能，並且陸續降低 32nm 等舊製程投片
量，將產能轉移至較為先進的 Flash 製程；高雄廠則預計滿載月產能將達 24~25K，
並透過製程轉換，提高 16nm 製程比重，進一步推成位元出貨量。投研部預估 2027
年營收約 6,008 億元，YoY+114.64%，毛利率 71.21%，YoY+3.3ppts，稅後淨利為
2,550 億元，YoY+128.84%，EPS 為 54.03 元。 
展望後市，產品應用的層面將隨著 Edge AI、On-device AI 以及 Physical AI 的興起
而顯著擴張，尤其在邊緣端設備上，NOR Flash 及 NAND Flash 的容量與數量需求都
將出現爆發性成長；DRAM 則將隨著前述 eSSD 的需求增加而帶動出貨成長。為了因
應 2028 年後更為龐大的市場需求，華邦已經通過 731 億元的資本支出案，正式啟動
下一階段高雄廠 Module B 的擴產計畫。該擴建工程預計 2029 年 1 月開始裝機，預
計於 2H29 小量出貨。Module B 的無塵室面積約為目前高雄廠的兩倍，滿載產能可
望達 50K 至 60K 的月產能，預計將採分階段裝機投產。製程方面，Module B 將採用
16 奈米、14 奈米及 12 奈米製程，並規劃在第二階段引進 EUV。 
此外，針對矽電容佈局方面，公司目前具備 5,400 nF/mm²超高容值規格的技術，具
備極薄化、耐高溫與優異的高頻反應特性。面對 AI 晶片先進封裝空間緊湊及傳統
MLCC 缺貨之痛點，華邦電矽電容可直接嵌入PCB 或 CoWoS 中介層，滿足光模組與
AI 加速晶片對高頻穩定供電的需求。公司採 Foundry 定位，目前產能已被 MLCC 及
AI 供應商包光，並已於高雄廠 Module B 規劃專屬線擴產，預估 2027 年後將成為
CUBE 之外的重要動能。 
CUBE 及矽電容陸續貢獻營收，中長期營運動能正向 
整體而言 ， 隨著華邦電4Gb DDR4/DDR3 出貨占比提升 ， 以及3Q26 陸續出貨的 8Gb 
DDR4 加入，可望改善產品組合，配合報價漲勢提升獲利表現；Flash 方面除了前述
受供給面影響而使報價呈上行趨勢外，預期華邦電將持續維持全球第一大 NOR Flash
供應商的領導地位，並受惠於長期伺服器與 AI 高容量應用的滲透率提升，同時隨車
用 、 消費性電子產品規格升級 ， 對於NOR Flash 或 SLC NAND 的需求亦見同步增長 ，
故公司於 2026 年再度追加 NOR 及 NAND 的產能擴充資本支出，顯見其對於後市正

### Page 4

- 4 -

個股報告 
向看待；最後，在中長期方面，CUBE 目前已進入與客戶合作開發階段，主要針對 AI 
ASIC 及邊緣運算需求，其優勢在於採用3D 堆疊技術，4 層堆疊的頻寬可對標 HBM，
但功耗僅為其 20%，極具競爭力，預計將於 2027 年開始顯著貢獻營收，同時公司新
增矽電容業務，預計將成為公司長期成長的關鍵動能。 
評價方面，考量公司 3Q26 ASP 漲勢優於先前預期，且 4Q26 Flash 產品仍具上漲空
間，再加上 2027 年位元出貨量可望迎來顯著成長，營收成長動能強勁之餘，中長期
亦有 CUBE 以及矽電容營收貢獻，預期營運至 2028 年可望維持高成長態勢，故投研
部維持強力買進建議，目標價 205 元(2027F PE 4X)。

本次報告：法說會

瑞昱 (2379 TT) 
財報與展望均優於預期

永豐觀點 
目前股價持穩於前季法說時點，而公司在財報與展望(包括毛利率、營業費用控管)
似乎均有好轉現象，尤其毛利率似乎出現谷底回溫 (優於研究部原預期的內外承
壓)，又公司在網通/車載等業務持續進展，股價應有上升空間。 
 投資評價與建議 
中立調升至買進：由於財報與展望均優於預期，毛利率谷底回溫，營業費用謹慎控
管，故研究部上修獲利預估值，預估瑞昱2026 年稅後 EPS 30.64 元(原預估 27.66
元)，2027 年稅後 EPS 35.19 元(原預估 29.15 元)。研究部認為瑞昱(1) 身為晶片
大廠，在資源調度、產品完整度、晶片優化或議價能力，相對小廠更具競爭力 (或
許也有機會提高市場份額)，此似乎也體現於 3Q26 財報與 4Q26 展望中，(2) 中
長期受會網通/車載/伺服器/邊緣 AI 裝置涉足而帶動營運成長，故將評等由中立調
升至買進建議，目標價 915 元(以 26X2027 年 EPS 計算)。

ESG 評析 
瑞昱企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2025 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。

General Issue Categories and Disclosure Topics in our work.

### Page 2

2

瑞昱為網通晶片大廠 ：瑞昱為全球網通晶片大廠，若以公司營收排名為全球第八大
IC 設計廠商。瑞昱產品線含括 Ethernet、WiFi、藍芽、Switch 與 TV SoC 。網通
領域的參與廠商為博通、Marvell、高通與聯發科等，TV SoC 的參與廠商則包括聯
發科、聯詠等。 
3Q26 獲利優於預期：瑞昱 3Q26 營收 377.32 億元(+0.5%QoQ，+27.9% YoY)，
毛利率 47.4%，稅後獲利 41.26 億元(+8%QoQ，+20%YoY)，稅後 EPS 8 元，獲
利優於預期，主要是營收、毛利率與營業費用控管均較預期佳。瑞昱3Q26 財報細
項要點如下：(1) 營收季增 0.5%，優於研究部原預期的季減 3%，主要是網通業務
成長驅使，以及部分產品價格調漲，從而抵銷筆電/TV/遊戲機需求減緩的衝擊。若
究終端應用分拆，PC 因終端市場需求疲軟，瑞昱 3Q26 PC 比重由前季的 33%降
至 30%(3Q26 PC: 非 PC 比重為 30%:70%。(2) 毛利率由 2Q26 的 47%微幅增至
47.4%(優於研究部原預期將受製造成本上升而使毛利率承壓)，主要是產品結構變
化(高階產品比重增加 )，以及產品因應成本上揚而進行轉嫁。 (3) 營業費用季減
0.6%，反映公司在投資研發的同時，仍對營業費用謹慎控管。(4) 業外收益僅 6.06
億元(季減 3900 萬元)， 主要是包含金融資產評價損失 。(5) 瑞昱庫存天數雖由2Q26
的 107 天增至 3Q26 的 136 天，不過主要是供應鏈持續有不確定性，公司為確保
客戶供貨的連貫性，故多建置庫存因應。 
4Q26 營收降幅不會如同過往第四季顯著：瑞昱認為(1) 4Q26 營運審慎穩健，雖
第四季通常為營運淡季， 但隨產品結構往高階挪移， 又價格為反映製造成本上升而
仍有調漲事宜，故營收降幅較為平緩，其中PC 需求仍相對疲弱。(3) 2H26 毛利率
將逐步回溫。整體而言，研究處預估瑞昱 4Q26 營收 348.5 億元(-7.6%QoQ，
+32.6%YoY)，毛利率隨產品結構變化與轉嫁而略增至 47.6%，稅後獲利 35.34 億
元(-14%QoQ，+33%YoY)，稅後 EPS 6.86 元。

### Page 3

3

2027 年營運應仍能維持成長力道：研究部認為台系 IC 設計族群今明年將持續遭
遇內外交雜的挑戰(內有製造成本攀升、外有消費電子需求疲軟)，不過瑞昱身為晶
片大廠，在資源調度、產品優化與議價能力均相對同業更具競爭力，使得營運表現
應能優於產業狀態(其實從瑞昱財報亦能得證，公司即使 30%營收與 PC 相關，但
其 3Q26 獲利數字與 4Q26 展望均仍穩健)。研究部認為瑞昱動能來自下述因子驅
動：(1) 網通隨電信業者對 25GPON 架構進行結構性測試、乙太網路交換器受益
於硬體升級、消費者與中小企業路由器往Ｍesh/三頻配置/WiFi7 升級，並且企業
級網路管理需求與管理型交換器持續普及中，均將推升瑞昱網通業務長期穩健成
長。(2) WiFi7 滲透率提升。隨瑞昱客戶的新開案均基於 WiFi7 作設計，瑞昱預期
WiFi7 滲透率將由 2026 年的 30%攀升至 40%。WiFi 聯盟計畫 WiFi8 將於 06/2027
完成認證測試，並將於 09/2027 前正式啟動 WiFi8 認證，瑞昱預期 2H27 公司
WiFI8 產品可望步入量產，以利客戶搭載 WiFi8 產品更廣泛於 2028 年上市。(3) 
光通訊專注 112G/224G SerDes 開發(4nm 製程)，100G 光模組控制晶片將以雲
端、資料中心與電信網路為主要著墨項目，明年量產規模提升，並且 400G/800G 
PAM4 DSP預計 2H27 推出。(4) 電動車成長持續帶動瑞昱車載乙太網路進展，瑞
昱預期新車搭載乙太網路的滲透率已超過 50%。(5) 中長期邊緣 AI 業務(包括車載
/機器人/IOT)與高效能運算 (比如瑞昱導入乙太網路 /交換器/xPON/SSD/IO 橋接
器/USB hub/光通訊)，目標 2030 年營收比重達 30%。 
中立轉買進：由於財報與展望均優於預期，毛利率谷底回溫，營業費用謹慎控管，
故研究部上修獲利預估值，預估瑞昱2026 年營收 1466 億元(+19%YoY)，毛利率
47.91%，稅後獲利 157.9 億元(+7%YoY)，稅後 EPS 30.64 元(原預估 27.66 元)，
並預估 2027 年營收 1594 億元(+9%YoY)，毛利率 48.37%，稅後獲利 181.4 億
元(+15%YoY)，稅後 EPS 35.19 元(原預估 29.15 元)。研究部認為瑞昱(1) 身為晶
片大廠，在資源調度、產品完整度、晶片優化或議價能力，相對小廠更具競爭力(或
許也有機會提高市場份額)，此似乎也體現於 3Q26 財報與 4Q26 展望中，(2) 中
長期受會網通/車載/伺服器/邊緣 AI 裝置涉足而帶動營運成長，故將評等由中立調
升至買進建議，目標價 915 元(以 26X2027 年 EPS 計算)。

Document 122: 261030_gs_airtac

### Page 1

Key Data  _____________________________________

GS Forecast  __________________________________

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

What surprised us 
AirTAC reported 3Q26 results today (October 29th). 3Q OP was in 
line with our estimates, but was 5% higher than BBG consensus. 
OPM was 33.9% in 3Q26, largely in line with our estimates; although 
GM of 49.4% was higher than our estimates by 0.9ppt, it was offset 
by higher opex (NT$1,778mn vs. GSe of NT$1,669mn). Total 
revenue was largely in line with GSe but was 4% above consensus, as 
the company enjoyed solid demand from the electronics industry 
(33% YoY in 3Q26 vs. 20% YoY in 2Q26, which accounted for 28% of 
total 3Q26 revenue). 
What surprised us the most during the results call was the 
company’s solid guidance on industry growth (mentioned low single 
to mid single digit % YoY growth is sustainable for the longer term 
for the China pneumatic market) and the company further 
mentioned that it could outgrow the market by 10ppt YoY in 2027. 
Moreover, the company expects all 
electronics/automotive/machine tools/general 
machinery/textile/packaging to see double digit YoY growth in both 
2026/27, thanks to the China government’s ongoing stimulus 
policies. Management reiterated 2026 revenue guidance in Rmb 
terms to reach 20%+ YoY growth (could reach higher YoY in NT$ 
terms), and maintained 2026 OPM guidance of 34%.  
The company reiterated its utilization rate target for its linear guide 
business to reach 50% by 2026 end (vs. 40%+ in 3Q26). The 
company continues to see peers raise prices for their linear guide 
products, and management thinks the company is well-positioned 
to gain market share with its stable pricing and shorter product lead 
time (less than a month vs. a key competitor at 4 months, see here).  
Going into 4Q26, we expect revenue to reach NT$11,425mn (-1% 
QoQ,+26% YoY), as we expect solid YoY across nearly all end 
markets. Rising raw material cost remains a key risk, while the

### Page 3

company continues to maintain a stable pricing strategy within the China market (which 
accounts for 90%+ of total revenue); thus we expect GM to slightly decline to 48.7% 
(-0.7ppt QoQ and +1.2ppt YoY). We continue to see the company’s ongoing effort in 
improving internal efficiency, which could partially offset the negative impact from rising 
material cost. 
We hold our conservative though optimistic view on the pneumatic industry outlook, as 
we continue to see downside risk in consumer electronics shipments (smartphone, etc) 
and the negative impact from rising raw material cost to persist over the coming 
quarters. We expect the company to continue to gain share in the China pneumatic 
market with its stable pricing strategy as well as its shift towards higher-quality 
pneumatic products.

Investment view 
We reiterate our Buy rating on AirTAC as we expect the company will continue to 
outperform the overall pneumatic market. We are positive on the company’s latest 
comment that the overall China pneumatic market YoY growth could be sustained at low 
single to mid single digit for a longer period, and management further mentioned that 
the company will outperform the market by 10ppt in 2027. This is mainly driven by the 
company’s latest guidance that nearly all end markets could see double digit YoY growth 
in 2027. While the company did mention the risk of decelerating YoY growth for the 
battery industry (which has seen solid YoY growth over the past two years), we expect 
solid growth from smaller segments, including machine tool/general 
machinery/textile/packaging, could offset the risk (we expect the 4 segments to account 
for 24% of total 2027E revenue vs. battery at 13%). 
We believe AirTAC is well positioned to overcome potential industry demand risks with 
its strong B/S and healthy cash flow position, which could further support market share 
gains during industry downturns (historically, AirTAC has accelerated its market share 
gains during industry downcycles, supported by its solid B/S and cash flow 
management). We also expect the company’s ROE to continue to improve in the coming 
years (ROE should improve to 26.2% in 2028E per GSe vs. 16%-17% in 2022-25) 
supporting a high dividend payout ratio (50%-60%+ per year per GSe). 
As a result, we maintain our Buy rating on AirTAC with unchanged 12m TP of NT$1,895.

Earnings and TP revisions 
We revise up our 2027/28E revenue estimates by 1/1% to factor in the 
better-than-expected growth in the China pneumatic market. We revise up our 2026E 
OPM by 0.3ppt to factor in actual 3Q26 results and revise up our 2027/28E OPM by 
0.3/0.1ppt as we continue to expect the company to focus on enhancing internal 
efficiency. We revise down our 2026E EPS estimates by 4% after factoring in a higher tax 
rate in 2H26. 
Also, we introduce our 2029E earnings estimates in this report. 
Maintain our Buy rating and 12m TP of NT$1,895, which is based on unchanged P/E of 
26x on our 2027E EPS, as we believe the company could trade at 0.25x STDV above the 
average PE multiple during the previous pneumatic upcycle.

### Page 6

Investment Thesis - AirTAC
AirTAC is the 2nd-largest pneumatic supplier in mainland China with 28% market share
in 2025, outpacing most local competitors, owing to its low-cost yet high quality
product, and service quality reputation in most key cities in mainland China. We forecast
its market share will reach 30% in 2026E and expect AirTAC to be the structural winner
in a deflationary market, due to its: (1) strong market growth and leadership: AirTAC

continues to expand its market share in mainland China driven by competitive pricing
that is more than 10% lower vs. international peers (with 45%+ GM / 30-35% OPM) and
high demand elasticity among mainland China customers; and (2) superior cost structure
and financial health: with best-in-class cash cost and a solid balance sheet, featuring
positive net cash over FCF since 2023/2024, we believe AirTAC is well-equipped to
weather industry downturns and capitalize on market restructuring.

Overall, we believe AirTAC will see better revenue/profitability in the long term given its
share gainer position in the China pneumatic market and potential to gain share in the
linear guide market (improved demand in the next 1-2 years). This should lead to
strengthened valuation, vs. its recent trading range (trading below its 10-year avg. P/E
multiple). We are Buy rated on AirTAC.
Price target risks and methodology - AirTAC
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$1,895 is based on a target P/E of 26.0x on

our 2027E EPS (0.25x STDV above avg of the last pneumatic upcycle).
Key downside risks: (1) Stronger competition from peers; (2) sharper-than-expected
Rmb depreciation, and (3) a longer-than-expected end-market demand downtrend in
mainland China.

other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We update our view on Advantech after 3Q26 results 
(released on 4 November).

What’s the impact: 3Q26 results beat. Advantech’s 3Q26 earnings of 
TWD6.2bn (EPS: TWD7.15) beat our estimate and BBG consensus by 
26% and 28%, respectively, driven by record-high OPM (20.9%) and better-
than-expected non-ops (gains from its Japan plant’s asset disposal). 
However, GM fell 1pp QoQ to 36.7%, mainly due to raw-material cost 
inflation. Management said CPU, memory and other component costs rose 
to 70% (vs. 30% previously). Although the company could pass on higher 
costs, it couldn’t maintain its blended GM on an incremental sales.

Business update. Looking ahead, Advantech remains positive on its 4Q26 
and 2027 outlook, supported by a c.USD2.5bn order backlog and a 3Q26 
B/B ratio of 1.64 (vs. 1.6 in October). Management said cost-inflation-driven 
demand pull-ins were limited in 9M26, but order visibility has extended as 
clients seek greater allocations. Advantech sees AI infrastructure, 
semiconductors, factory automation and medical equipment as key growth 
drivers, with shipments in these applications expected to grow c.15% YoY 
in 2026E and into 2027E, based on our forecasts. Edge AI remains its 
fastest-growing application: its revenue contribution rose to 24.5% in 9M26, 
with c.109% YoY growth, vs. 22.5% in 1H26 and the company’s c.30% 
target for 2026. Growth has been driven mainly by higher ASPs, but we 
expect unit growth to become the primary driver in 2027E as ASPs lap the 
increase in 2026 already. In the near term, while management guides its 
revenue would decrease by single-digit % QoQ due to fewer working days 
over Christmas holiday period in 4Q26, but we believe this is too 
conservative. We expect 4Q26E revenue to be broadly flat QoQ, as a solid 
backlog is on hand. We also expect gross margin to remain similar to 3Q26 
amid ongoing cost inflation. Management reported ASP increases of 10-
20% for CPUs and 15-20% for PCBs, while SSD and memory prices have 
been relatively stable recently. We expect shortages to ease gradually, 
particularly for memory, but to persist for CPUs and CCLs.

What we recommend: We raise our 2026-28E EPS by 7-22% to reflect 
Advantech’s 3Q26 results. We reaffirm our Outperform (2) call and raise 
our 12M TP to TWD750 (from TWD700), based on a lower PER of 26x 
(prior: 30x; slightly higher than the mid-point of its past-3-year range of 20-
37x), as we are closer to the end of the up-cycle, and applied to our 1-year 
forward EPS. Key downside risk: slower-than-expected demand recovery.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 10-17% above the consensus, 
likely due to our more bullish outlook on Advantech’s revenue.

4 November 2026

3Q26 results: positive tone towards 2027

➢ Up-cycle will last into 2027, with limited early pull-in shipments in 2026 
➢ Key growth drivers in 2026 include ASP hike and fast Edge AI growth 
➢ Reaffirming our Outperform (2) rating; raising 12-month TP to TWD750

Company profile 
Founded in 1981 and listed in 1999, Advantech is the largest industrial PC (IPC) maker worldwide in 
terms of embedded board/system sales, followed by the company’s main competitors, Germany-based 
Kontron and US-based Radisys. The company mainly provides embedded computing boards, chassis, 
industrial computers, applied computers and design-in services. The businesses are categorised into 4 
segments: Embedded design-in, Industrial IoT, Service IoT, and Global services.

### Page 3

Chroma ATE (2360.TW)
05 November 2026 Citi Research
3
Chroma ATE
Valuation
Our target price of NT$2,900 is based on 40x 2027E P/E, at close to 1x PEG. Chroma shares have re-rated above its historical
range as its growth outlook is now strongly tied to AI infrastructure build out. We estimate 60%+ of its revenue are now AI-
related, with strong growth across product segments including power testers, SLT, metrology, and photonics. Chroma has a
commanding market share in most of these segments and well positioned to capitalized on the global AI expansion.

Risks
Downside risks to achieving our target price include: 1) softer AI/CPO growth expectation due to changes in capex outlook or
production bottleneck; 2) fewer SLT needed as a result of shortened cycle time as GPU yield rate improves; 3) disappointing
metrology shipment due to changes at ramp-up schedule; 4) testing process change leading to loss in market share; 5) FX risks
(50-60% of Chroma’s sales are in US$, 10-20% in Rmb).
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保

1 
6515 穎崴(WinWay)

報告日期： 2026/11/03 研 究 員：翁浩軒  (Davy Wong)  
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要： 
 AI 晶片帶動高階測試需求暢旺。 
 產品組合轉佳加上探針自製比重提升有助毛利率回升。 
 2026 下半年及 2027 上半年新增產能，帶動今明年營收創高。

一. 3Q26 毛利率有望回升，EPS 27.63 元 
穎崴 3Q26 營收 45.56 億(QoQ+29.32%、YoY+152.58%)，營收再創
單季歷史新高 ， 受惠於 AI 晶片對 Socket 需求暢旺 ， 加上 Probe Card
拉貨挹助，預期毛利率在 Probe Card 比重降低下回升至 39.7%，費
用率降至 13%，稅後淨利 10.07 億(QoQ+49.8%、YoY+170.86%)，
EPS 27.63 元。

二、4Q26 營收季持平，預估 2026 年 EPS 95.86 元 
展望 4Q26，儘管 Socket 需求持續放量，但在營收基期已高，加上
Probe Card 案拉貨放緩下，預估營收將季持平，而毛利率在產品組
合優化加上探針自製比重提升下預估將升至 41.3% 。 預估穎崴 4Q26
營收 46.2 億(QoQ+1.4%、YoY+106.84%)，毛利率 41.3%，稅後淨利
11.08 億(QoQ+10.01%、YoY+129.20%)，EPS 30.4 元。預估 2026 年
營收 156.8 億(YoY+99.57%)，毛利率 40.49%，稅後淨利 34.86 億
(YoY+108.36%)，EPS 95.86 元。

三.  2027 年新產能挹助獲利創高，預估 EPS 187.56 元 
展望 2027 年，AI 對高階測試需求仍強勁，晶片製程複雜性增加，
將帶動 Probe Card、FT/SLT/Burn-in Socket 及 Thermal 解決方案需
求，穎崴為多家 AI 晶片廠供應商，營收獲利可望持續創高。客戶
別 而 言，美 系 GPU、CPU、TPU 對 Socket 需 求 持 續，CPU Probe Card
將持續貢獻穩。另外公司切入 CPO 後段測試，目前於客戶端驗證
中，隨著未來訊號將透過光做高速傳輸，穎崴 CPO 測試解決方案
有望成為中長線新的成長動能。公司 2027 年 Socket 及探針產能預
估較 2026 年初翻倍，且隨自製探針比重提升，有助毛利率改善。
預估穎崴 2027 年營收 266.23 億(YoY+69.78%)，毛利率 42.45%，稅
後淨利 68.36 億(YoY+96.12%)，EPS 187.56 元。

四.投資建議：買進，目標價 6000 元 
穎崴深耕半導體測試介面、探針卡、精密彈簧針及溫控模組技術
投資評等：買進 
目標價位：6000 元

2 
研發多年，為高效能 IC 測試治具的半導體測試介面設備商，公司
在高頻高速同軸測試座市場具領先地位，且與多家全球 IC 設計領
導廠商合作密切，在客戶研發階段即參與測試解決方案之開發。
隨著 AI 需求帶動半導體先進封裝需求放量，高階晶片所需的測試
種類、測試時間、複雜度均提升，均使穎崴測試治具及探針卡出
貨成長。考量到 1)晶片測試需求長期成長、2)公司毛利率可望落
底回升、3)2027 年新產能挹助帶動公司獲利創高，目前評價偏低，
參考 2027 年 EPS 給予 32 倍本益比，目標價 6000 元，建議買進。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
穎崴成立於 2001 年 4 月，總部位於高雄楠梓加工出口區，主要從
事半導體測試治具(Test Socket)及探針卡(Probe Card)之設計、開發
及生產製造關鍵技術，從晶圓測試(CP)、最終測試(FT)、系統測試
(SLT)、老化測試(Burn-In)及溫控系統(Thermal Control System)，提
供各種高速、高頻、廣溫域的測試介面；主要客戶為全球高階 IC
設計公司及封測大廠，為提供高效能 IC 測試治具的專業半導體測
試介面製造商。公司產品包括半導體測試介面、精密彈簧針、探
針卡、溫控模組，2026 上半年之營收比重分別為：測試治具 64%、
探針卡 27%、接觸元件 6%、其他 3%。公司為全球第一大邏輯測
試座供應商，主要競爭對手為日韓歐美廠商，國內同業包括精測、
旺矽、雍智科技等。

二.產業趨勢概況  
1. AI 帶動半導體測試需求 
受惠於 AI / HPC 需求爆發 2.5D/3D 先進封裝、Chiplet 架構應用快
速導入市場，晶片複雜度提升且封裝面績增加下，晶片尺寸放大
且測試接腳數亦快速增加 ， 另外晶片大廠在 AI GPU 、 CPU 、 與 ASIC
迭代速度加快，均使晶片測試規格提升且測試時間增加，帶動最
終測試（FT）、系統級測試（SLT）與老化測試（Burn-in）耗材需
求需求長期成長。穎崴深耕半導體測試介面、探針卡、精密彈簧
針及溫控模組技術多年，為高效能 IC 測試治具的半導體測試介面
領導廠商。除了後段測試，因應先進封裝成本昂貴，若在封裝後
才發現晶片缺陷將面臨巨大報廢損失，因此半導體廠加強在晶圓
切割前即進行晶片測試， 確認為 KGD ， 穎崴近年亦跨入 Probe Card
領域，與探針卡大廠 Technoprobe 簽訂 MEMS 探針卡供貨合約，
亦使探針卡營收比重近兩年提升至 20~30%水準。

2. 半導體測試介面市場高速成長  
公司的產品提供測試時所需要的硬體介面，稱為測試治具(Test 
Socket)，是 Device 與測試板之間的一個介面，結構中含有重要的
接觸電性元件(Contact Element)：Spring Probe，Spring Probe 提供
Device 和測試板(Test Board)間的訊號傳遞 ， 用於 FT/SLT 之 Socket ，
隨著 AI 晶片使用更多的 SLT 測試，將帶動公司的 Hyper Socket、
Coaxial Socket 、 Thermal System 需求 。根據研調機構預估 ，SLT&SFT 
2026~2030 CAGR 高於 15%。

3. 穎崴走在技術前端，與客戶共同未來需求產品  
穎崴與多家全球 IC 設計領導廠商合作密切，在客戶研發階段即參
與測試解決方案之開發，直接受惠於先進封裝、矽光共同封裝等
趨勢。公司之同軸測試座（Coaxial Socket）為目前 AI/HPC 晶片的

主流測試介面，支援最高 224Gbps 數據傳輸速率及高熱功耗處理
能力; 新一代超導測試座 (HyperSocket)提供晶片更柔和的接觸介
面，能顯著提高測試穩定性，同時實現阻抗精準匹配，有效降低
串擾，並大幅提升耐電流能力，亦可延長探針使用壽命並降低更
換成本 ， 將應用於未來大尺寸封裝與高 Pin 數晶片測試; 另外公司
亦提供多樣化測試座產品線，包括可耐受廣溫域及高功率的老化
測試座（Burn-in Socket） 與 PoP &塑膠測試座 。 共同封裝光學（CPO）
方面，公司全面布局 CPO 測試流程，主要著墨於 Die Level 測試，
推出雙面測試系統與光電測試座，在 M o d u l e  L e v e l  則以  
HyperSocket 切入。

4. 高雄楠梓新廠自製針產能持續開出  
隨著半導體需求暢旺，晶片測試複雜度提升，高階測試產品拉貨
強勁，公司積極擴大產能，新增產能主要來自仁武租賃廠房及楠
梓廠區，仁武二廠已動土，總面積 6300 坪(為一廠的 6 倍)，預估
2Q27 開始量產 。 今明年新產能包括探針及 Test Socket ， 探針部分，
探針 1H26 600 萬針/月，2H26 達 900 萬針/月，1H27 將達 1400 萬針
/月，除了滿足客戶需求，規模放大有助平均成本下降，自製比重
增加亦有助毛利率提升，Socket 產能方面，1H26 產能 3.3K 組/月，
2H26 達 4.2K 組/月，1H27 達 7.2K 組/月。公司未來仍將依市場需
求持續擴產，預估至 2030 年產能逐年提升，公司在後段測試元件
產能將維持領導地位。

 절대 (달러환산) 6.1  13.8  (8.6)

3Q26Re: 신규 시설 매출 인식 개시 
연결 기준 매출액 1조 4,903억원(+18.5%YoY, 이하 YoY 생략), 영업이익 6,401억원
(+1.0%, OPM 42.9%)로 컨센서스를 상회하는 실적 기록. 1~4공장이 완전 가동을 유지하는 
한편, 5공장은 PPQ 배치 생산 본격화되면서 매출 기여 확대. 상업 생산 전환이 예상되는 
2H27부터 이익 기여 커질 것으로 기대. 
연초 인수한 Rockville 공장은 3Q에도 안정적인 상업 생산을 이어갔으며 2Q 생산 물량의 
매출이 인식되며 매출 성장에 기여. 동사는 Rockville 공장 매출 인식 등을 반영하여 기존 
+15~20% 성장에서 +20%로 연간 가이던스 상향.   
고객사 수주 회복 기대 
3Q26 기준 누적 수주액은 219억 달러로 전분기 대비 소폭 증가했으나 과거 연간 수주액 
증가 대비 부족한 수준. 저조한 수주 실적은 지난해 초 의약품에 대한 관세 불확실성 증가로 
고객사들의 수주 문의가 저조했던 영향. 26년 초부터 관세 불확실성이 낮아지며 수주 문의
는 회복 추세. 약 1년 정도 논의 기간이 소요되는 것을 고려할 때 26년말~27년 초 수주 회
복 확인 가능할 것으로 기대. 
안정적인 공급망 유지가 중요한 CDMO 산업 특성상 노조 파업 가능성도 고객사 수주를 늦
추는 요인으로 판단. 다만, 10/16 인천 고등법원이 쟁의행위 가처분 항고심에 대해 인용 결
정을 하며 기존 3개 공정에서 9개 공정으로 확대되며 파업 우려는 다소 완화. 
투자 의견 Buy, 목표 주가 200만원 유지 
매수 의견과 목표 주가 200만원을 유지. 원달러 약세에도 불구하고 5공장 Ramp-up과 
Rockville 공장 매출 기여로 견조한 실적 성장 지속될 것으로 예상. 파업 이슈 해소와 대형 
수주 계약이 주가 상승을 이끌 것으로 판단.

### Page 1

LG에너지솔루션 (373220)

Earnings Review Company Analysis | 이차전지/화학 | 2026. 11. 4  
일회성 보상금 포함된 실적, ‘30년 ESS 수요 대비 배 이상 높은 셀 3사 Capa 우려

AMPC 4,169억원 포함 영업이익 컨센서스 대폭 상회 
동사는 8일 매출액 9조 6,434억원(YoY 59%, QoQ 27%), AMPC 포함한 영업이익 
7,560억원(OPM 7.8%, YoY 26%, QoQ 567%)로 시장 Consensus(공정공시한 8일 
기준 3,264억원)을 대폭 상회하는 어닝 서프라이즈 발표. 회사는 ESS 출하 급증과 고
전압 Mid-Ni EV 파우치 확대, 견조한 EV향 2170-46시리즈 공급을 물량 중심을 성
장하였고, 폴란드 가동률 개선, 수익성이 견조한 원통형 물량 증가, 북미 ESS 생산성 
개선에 따른 고정비 부담 완화 및 고객사의 일회성 물량 보상금 영향으로 설명. 그러나 
일회성 물량 보상금의 구체적 금액, 반복 발생 여부는 제시하지 않아 불확실성 남겨. 
7,560억원의 영업이익에도 불구하고 영업외손실 9,872억원 발생으로 당기순손실 기
록. 이에는 소형 IT파우치 자산손상 반영되어, 가동률 개선 및 감가상각비 감소를 위해 
Capa를 조정하는 배터리 회사들의 대응 전략이 이번 실적에서 다시 확인

‘30년 ESS 수요 전망들 대비 국내사 Capa 배 이상 높아 
동사는 북미 ESS Capa 확대를 통한 탈중국 수요에 적극 대응하겠다는 전략. 파우치 
라인 추가 전환 및 신규 각형 라인 확보를 통해 북미 ESS Capa를 ‘26년말 50+에서 
‘27년 70+로 제시. 최근 타사에서 ‘26년 미국 ESS 수요를 80~100GWh, 혹은 
130+GWh로, ‘30년 두배 이상, 혹은 240+GWh로 수요 전망에 대해 답변한 바 있음. 
동사 전체 Capa 300GWh(‘25년 연말), SK On 179GWh, SDI 140GWh)임을 감안하
면 ‘30년 약 2배 수요를 가정하면 셀 3사 Capa의 32%에 달하는 수요, 만일 3배 수
요 가정하면 48.5% 수준. 북미 외 시장 가성비 중시 기조 및 중국 LFP 장악(‘25년 
97% M/S)으로 진입장벽 높음을 감안하면 미국 수요 중요. 이를 상쇄하기 위해 EV향 
배터리의 안정적인 공급 지속 필요하며, 이를 위해 동사 원통형은 TESLA의 비미국 시
장 판매량이, 파우치는 시장 자체 축소 지속 여부에 유의할 필요 
상대적으로 가장 견조한 출하량, 그러나 고평가 영역  
셀3사중 EV향 배터리 출하량 및 ESS 탑재에서 현재 가장 견조한 판매량을 보이고 있
으나, AMPC와 일회성 보상금이 영업손익의 상당부분을 견인한다는 점에서 수익성 취
약하다는 단점. 또한 ROE가 (-) 영역 임에도 PBR이 3.x내외로 거래되고 있어 고평가 
지적에서 자유롭기 어려워. 당분간 모회사인 LG화학의 PRS 발행에 따른 단기 오버행 
이슈도 관건. PBR 2.3 x(‘25년대비 하락하는 ROE 감안, 20% 할인)적용 목표주가 
290,000원 제시, HOLD 유지. 주식 투자 매력 낮은 상황으로 판단 
 Analyst 정경희 
khchung@ls-sec.co.kr

LS Securities Research 2 
EV향 3분기 data  
이차전지에서 EV향 비중은 여전히 가장 높다. 타입별로 보면 3분기 글로벌 EV향 이차전
지에서 각형의 M/S가 83%로 지속 증가하고 있으며, 동사가 생산하는 원통형과 파우치의 
경우 합산 M/S가 17%로 하락하였다. 현재 Wh 기준 동사 EV향 배터리에서 3분기 파우
치가 55%, 원통형이 45%로 파우치 비중이 10% 원통형을 상회한다. 
그림1 LGES 원통형, 파우치 타입 EV 향 분기별 탑재량 추이 
그림2

자료: SNE Research(2026.10), LS증권 리서치센터

그림 2 EV 향 폼팩터 M/S: 각형 확장 vs 원통형/파우치 감소  그림 3 동사 EV 배터리 타입 비중

자료: SNE Research(2026.10), LS증권 리서치센터  자료: SNE Research(2026.10), LS증권 리서치센터

SNE data에 따르면 글로벌 원통형 EV향 수요의 대다수가 TESLA에서 발생하고 있으며, 
‘24~’25년 역성장후 ‘26년 판매량 회복을 나타내는 TESLA 호조로 전년대비 LGES의 EV
향 원통형 판매량은 3분기 증가했다. 반면 파우치의 경우 ‘25년 파우치 탑재 EV 판매 호조
를 나타낸 현대기아차그룹 영향으로 YoY 탑재량이 증가했으나, 올해는 북미 OEM인 GM, 
Ford향 판매가 IRA 일몰로 전년대비 부진함에 따라 3분기 전년대비 25% 감소한 것으로 
나타난다.

LS Securities Research 4 
그림 9 셀 3 사 분기별 EV 향 탑재량 글로벌 M/S 추이: M/S 감소중

자료: SNE 리서치, LS증권 리서치센터

밸류에이션, 수익성 대비 고평가 영역  
동사는 ‘24년 ROE -4.9%, ‘25년 -5.2%에 이어 ‘26년 -4.4%, ‘27년 -3.4%로 (-) 영역이 
지속되는 것으로 예상한다. ‘24년과 ‘25년 순이익은 (+)지만, 해외 합작회사가 많고 이들 이
익을 연결함에 따라 지배주주순이익은 적자를 기록한 것을 보인다. JV 규모가 축소되고 있
어 이러한 순이익과 지배주주 순이익상의 괴리는 좁혀지겠지만, ROE가 (-)영역 지속을 예
상함에 따라 현재 거래 PBR은 고평가 상황으로 볼 수 있다. 물론 이것은 향후 수익 성장
에 대한 시장 기대가 반영된 것으로 볼 수 있지만, 이차전지 6개사 평균 Consensus 대비 
실제 수익이 ‘23년부터 지속 하향되고 있다는 점, ESS대비 여전히 판매비중이 높은 EV에서 
출하량 정체가 일어나고 있다는 점, 미국 ESS에서 여전히 높은 중국 M/S를 감안하면 이러
한 기대 수준에 대해 다시 검토할 필요가 있다. 이에 더해, 모회사 기준에서는 필요한 과정
이지만 동사 주가 측면에서는 LG화학의 PRS 발행도 단기 오버행으로 당분간 주가에 부담
을 줄 수 있다.

그림 10 셀 3 사, 양∙음극재 3 사 추정치 변화율: ‘23 년부터 추정치 지속 하향 조정

자료: DataGuide, LS증권 리서치센터

미국 ESS가 모든 것을 해결할 수 있나? 
‘25년 글로벌 ESS 시장의 97%를 중국배터리사가 공급했다. LFP 각형이 표준화된 시장인 
것이다. 미국 시장만 중국에 대한 관세 허들, 정책 허들로 한국 배터리사에 기회로 작용한
다. 그러나 ‘26년부터 시작된 FEOC와 관세추가 부담에도 불구, 상반기 미국 ESS 시장의 
76%을 (기타사의 국적을 알 수 없기에 제외하더라도) 중국 셀사가 차지했다. 만일 한국 배
터리 유관회사들이 전망하듯 ‘30년 미국 ESS 시장이 160~200GWh 이상, 240+GWh, 혹
은 240~300GWh가 된다고 해도 3사 전체 Capa의 50%가 채 되지 못한다. EV 수요가 뒷
받침되지 않는다면 미국 ESS만으로는 가동률을 80~90% 이상 올리기 어렵다는 의미다. 특
히 동사는 원통형과 파우치를 생산하고 있으나, 원통형은 북미외 지역 TESLA의 판매에, 
파우치는 올해 출하량이 감소하는 것으로 나타나고 있다. 파우치 고객의 각형 채택 비중 
증가를 감안하면 동사 EV향 배터리 출하량 증가를 확신하기는 어렵다.   
긍정적 기대와 동시에 냉정한 점검이 필요한 시점으로 판단한다.

표2 LG 에너지솔루션 3Q26 실적발표 주요 Q&A 
[Q&A]           
Q1-1. 4Q26 실적 전망과 2027년 큰 방향은? 
  A. 4Q26 ESS 출하량은 북미 수요 대응으로 QoQ 약 40% 증가 예상 
   유럽 고전압 Mid-Ni 파우치 및 원통형 물량도 연말까지 견조한 출하 지속 전망 
   3Q26 가동을 시작한 북미 JV 효과가 더해져 전사 매출은 QoQ 약 10% 성장 가능할 것으로 예상 
   가동률 개선과 사이트 안정적 램프업은 지속 추진하나 3Q26 일회성 요인 소멸의 기저효과 존재 
   4Q26 혼다 JV ESS CAPA 추가 가동 준비에 따른 초기 고정비 부담으로 수익성 일부 둔화 불가피

2027년 사업계획은 수립 중이며 전방 수요 변수로 구체적 가이던스 제시는 어려운 단계 
    ESS: 북미 확보 수주 기반으로 유의미한 물량 성장 지속 기대 
    소형: 전략 고객향 EV 원통형과 46 시리즈 신규 애플리케이션 판매로 안정적 성장 예상 
    EV 파우치: 중저가 제품 물량과 북미 EV 향 생산품이 더해져 성장 예상 
   전 사업부 매출 성장 기대하나 핵심 제품 경쟁력·기존 CAPA 효율화·가동률 극대화·원가 개선에 집중 
   2027년 매출 성장률·영업이익률 등 구체적 수치는 제시 X

Q1-2. 북미 PFE 요건 충족을 위한 LFP 공급망 전략과 중국 관련 주력 양극재 공급사 대응은? 
  A. 미국 생산세액공제 확보를 위해 직접재료비 내 PFE 소재 비중 요건을 충족하는 공급망 구축 필요 
   회사 설명상 PFE 소재 허용 비중은 2026년 40%에서 2027년 35% 이하로 강화. Non-PFE 비중과 혼동 유의 
   소재별 소싱 전략을 구체화해 준비 중이며 요건 충족은 차질 없이 진행될 것으로 판단 
   재료비 비중이 큰 양극재는 최근 계약한 국내 업체 외 복수 Non-PFE 업체 조달 옵션 확보

음극재·분리막·전해액도 기존 Non-PFE 공급망에 더해 신규 업체 적극 발굴 
   기존 원가 중심 최적화에서 정책·지정학 리스크를 고려한 공급망 안정성 강화로 전략 확대 
   협력사의 원가·기술·물량 대응력을 종합 평가하고 장기적으로 현지 협력사 발굴·현지화 추진 
   질문한 중국 관련 주력 공급사의 개별 지위·조달 비중에 대한 직접적인 상세 답변 X

Q2-1. ESS 사업의 수익성 전망 및 개선 방안은? 
  A. EV→ESS 라인 전환으로 수요에 맞는 생산체계를 구축하고 북미 고객 적기 공급에 주력 
   다수 사이트 동시 램프업에 어려움이 있었고 고정비 절감 노력에도 수익성 개선 속도는 시장 기대보다 느렸음 
   2026년 말 50GWh 목표 셀 라인 램프업은 마무리 국면. Link CAPA도 3Q26 에 이어 4Q26 추가 보강 예정 
   CAPA 안정화와 점진적인 고정비 부담 완화 기대 
   확보 CAPA 효율화, 소재 소싱 다변화로 원재료비·물류비 절감에 집중 
   AI 데이터센터 및 SI 프로젝트 비중 확대를 통한 고객 포트폴리오 최적화 추진 
   셀·시스템 고용량화로 원가 경쟁력을 높여 구조적 수익성 개선 추진 
   구체적 ESS 영업이익률 목표나 개선 폭은 제시 X

Q2-2. 북미 ESS 추가 수주 현황과 향후 모멘텀은? 
  A. 빠른 시장 성장 및 Non-PFE 에 우호적인 정책으로 북미 현지 생산 셀 수요 급증 
   현재 계획된 CAPA 기준으로 2028년까지 물량은 대부분 확정. 그 이후 물량 중심으로 고객 수주 협의 진행 
   기존 전력망·유틸리티 고객, 이들을 통한 AI 데이터센터 BESS 수요, 하이퍼스케일러 BTM 직접 구매 수요 포함 
   예상 수요가 공급 가능 CAPA를 초과해 전략적으로 중요한 프로젝트 중심으로 미래 잔여 CAPA 운영 계획 
   추가 고객명·신규 계약량 등은 제시하지 않음. 예상 수요와 확정 수주를 구분

Q3-1. 46시리즈의 구체적인 수주 현황과 향후 모멘텀은? 
  A. 기존 OEM 부터 EV 스타트업까지 이미 440GWh 이상 물량 확보 
   추가로 다수 글로벌 OEM 과 협의 중이며 가까운 시일 내 의미 있는 규모의 수주 성과 기대 
   제품 경쟁력·지역별 생산 역량으로 46 시리즈 선도 입지를 강화하고 고객 포트폴리오 확대 계획 
   440GWh는 확보 물량이며 연간 판매량이나 생산 CAPA가 아님. 추가 계약 규모·고객명은 미공개

### Page 7

Company Report / LG에너지솔루션 / 2026. 11.4 
LS Securities Research 7

Q3-2. Tabless 2170의 경쟁력과 수주·신규 시장 대응 현황은? 
  A. 기존 제품 대비 낮은 발열·저항으로 안전성 및 고출력에 유리해 HEV·BBU·로봇 등 다양한 용도에 적합 
   오창에서 전동공구용 제품은 이미 양산 중  
   BBU 향 LFP 2170은 2Q27 양산 목표로 준비 중 
   HEV 향은 2027년 말 애리조나에서 양산 계획  
   LFP 2170은 NCM 대비 열안전성 테스트에서 우수한 성능을 보여 고객의 긍정적 피드백 확보 
   고객 니즈·수요에 적극 대응해 신시장 경쟁력 확보 추진. 수주 규모 등 정량 정보는 제시하지 않음

Q4-1. 재무 건전성 개선 방안과 2027 년 자금 조달 계획은? 
  A. 선제 CAPA 투자 자금은 저리 정책자금 장기차입 및 원화·외화 회사채로 안정적으로 조달해 왔음 
   주요 투자는 대부분 집행됐고 CAPEX는 2025년을 기점으로 유의미하게 감소 중 
   향후 영업현금흐름 확대분을 차입금 상환에 우선 활용, 자산 유동화를 통한 추가 재원 확보도 병행 
   차입금·금융비용 부담을 축소해 재무지표 개선 및 건전성 확보 추진 
   2026년 본사 추가 차입 계획은 없으며 해외법인은 기존 약정 차입금 일부 추가 인출 예정 
   2027년은 기존 차입금 차환·운전자금 범위에서 최소한의 자금 조달 계획 
   영업현금 확대·투자 부담 완화에 따른 잉여 재원으로 차입금 감축 기조 지속. 구체적 조달 규모는 미제시

Q4-2. 유럽 IAA 정책 방향에 대응한 중장기 점유율 확보 전략은? 
  A. IAA는 아직 최종안 미확정이나 수정 권고안상 외국인 직접투자·현지화 요건 강화 예상 
   배터리뿐 아니라 주요 소재의 현지 생산 역량 중요성이 확대될 전망 
   유럽 OEM 의 공급망 다변화로 현지 생산 체계를 갖춘 비중국 업체 소싱 니즈 확대, 경쟁 환경은 우호적 판단 
   폴란드 현지 양산 및 소재·부품 공급망을 이미 구축해 현지 배터리 소싱 수요에 신속 대응 가능 
   고전압 Mid-Ni 에 대한 유럽 고객 관심 증가. 폴란드 감가상각비 감소도 가격 경쟁력 확보에 기여 
   고전압 Mid-Ni 외 LFP·CTP 등 다양한 제품·솔루션으로 신규 수주를 확보해 유럽 점유율 확대 추진

Document 265: Advanced economies _ Resilience to shock after shock (2026-09-24)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Advanced economies _ Resilience to shock after shock (2026-09-24).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-09-24
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

3
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoPerspectives / 3rd Quarter 2026 / September 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
History tends to repeat itself in advanced economies. Once again, growth ultimately fell short of expectations by only a small
margin in the first half of 2026, despite the conflict in Iran. As early as 2025, the impact of tariffs was less severe than feared.
This is a sign that structural factors (notably AI, defence and electrification) are underpinning growth in these countries

and are expected to continue to do so in 2027. However, there are significant risks to the economic outlook, primarily of a
geopolitical nature, with a significant upside risk to inflation and interest rates. The other instance of history repeating itself
concerns a period that those under 20 cannot have experienced: the period leading up to the 2008 crisis, echoes of which can
be seen in terms of growth, inflation, the direction of monetary policy, and long-term interest rates.

Advanced economies are experiencing trends similar to those they
faced nearly 20 years ago:
- Growth remains resilient in 2025-26 despite shocks, as it did in 2006-
07, in the major advanced economies.
- Inflation is above central banks’ targets: as in 2006-07, this calls for a
tightening of monetary policy.
- In Europe and the United States, long-term interest rates have re -
turned to their 2006-07 levels (1996 in Japan): a challenging environ-

ment for public finances in a context where public debt ratios are signi-
ficantly higher than those seen 20 years ago.
THE GLOBAL ECONOMY IN THE MIDST OF A GROWTH CYCLE
We are already well into 2026, and the picture so far is clear: glo -
bal growth is proving resilient. Following a US tariff shock last year,
growth is once again proving, for the time being, to be significantly less
affected by the conflict in Iran than had been anticipated when it first

broke out. Initially, however, there were fears that this new shock would
hit growth even harder. As with the tariff hike in 2025, the impact was
measurable, particularly in terms of consumer confidence. But in both
2025 and 2026, growth has also shown a degree of resilience.
The figures for the second quarter bear this out, despite the sharp rise
in oil prices, the recessionary impact of which is not yet apparent.
According to our forecasts, growth was expected to reach or exceed 1%

in 2026 in the four major advanced economies (the Eurozone, Japan,
the United Kingdom and the United States, where it exceeds 2%) for
the second consecutive year. This had not happened (excluding the
post-COVID catch-up period) since 2006-07. Growth figures are more
moderate (particularly for demographic reasons), but the private sec -
tor is not (at present) showing the same excesses (at that time, the
credit boom had preceded the worst crisis since the 1930s); although

debt levels have recently risen in the US tech sector.
The driving force behind the current growth cycle is clear: artificial
intelligence (AI). It is fuelling global trade and business investment
in most countries. This is clearly the case in the United States, where
business investment is growing strongly as investment plans in the
US tech sector are implemented.
However, the world’s major economies are currently engaged in a
broader push to expand their capital goods investment. Alongside the

ongoing development of services—particularly digital services—we
have seen a rebound in industrial production in most countries since
early 2025. The leading sectors are primarily technology equipment,
machinery, aeronautics, defence and their essential inputs (energy and
metals). This trend is evident everywhere, including in Europe, and
is becoming entrenched over the long term, with an improvement in
business climate indicators, particularly new export orders. Growth is

therefore expected to strengthen further in 2027, particularly in the
Eurozone, where German investment plans are set to gather momen -
tum and whose spillover effects will have a greater impact on the eco-
nomy.
However, not all sectors are experiencing the same momentum. This
is particularly true for the sectors that depend on household consump-
tion, which has shown mixed trends. Our observation that consumer
spending is more buoyant in the United States than in Europe was

confirmed in 2026, and the outlook does not point to a change in this
ranking.
EDITORIAL
RESILIENCE TO SHOCK AFTER SHOCK

### Page 2

4
EcoPerspectives / 3rd Quarter 2026 / September 2026 economic-research.bnpparibas.com
The bank
for a changing
world
While European and US households are experiencing more or less the
same energy price shock, inflation remains higher in the United States.
But this has not prevented consumer spending from keeping pace, pri-
marily because it is driven by the wealthiest households, who are less
sensitive to inflation than to the wealth effect. Furthermore, while the

savings rate remains high in the Eurozone, US households have been
drawing down their savings. This is also true of British households,
which explains why the UK has seen slightly stronger growth since
the beginning of the year. In Japan, government spending has partially
offset energy inflation (while other countries are doing so to a much
lesser extent than in 2022), which has supported consumption growth.
However, these buffers—reduced savings or fiscal support—have their

limits when shocks persist. The two major current conflicts (Iran and
Ukraine) are far from being resolved, and their impact is continuing.
This persistent geopolitical risk and its implications for inflation repre-
sent a significant headwind to the growth outlook for 2027.
HIGHER INFLATION CALLS FOR MONETARY TIGHTENING
Inflation is exceeding the target set by three of the four central banks
examined in this publication, which is another similarity (albeit with

different underlying causes) with the 2006-07 period. Japan is the ex -
ception, as government support measures for households are tempo -
rarily holding back inflation.
Apart from the Bank of Japan, which is engaged in a long-term norma-
lisation process, no central bank was expected to raise its key rates
in 2026. But they are now being prompted to do so for reasons that
vary from one region to another. The first is the conflict in Iran and
the resulting rise in oil prices, which triggered a rebound in energy

inflation from the second quarter onwards. However, since the conflict
was not resolved by the memorandum of understanding signed in June,
and given that the subsequent drop in oil prices proved short-lived,
inflation is expected to rise again. The rise in gas prices is expected to
exacerbate the impact, particularly in Europe, where inventories are
being replenished ahead of winter. Electricity prices are expected to
follow suit in most countries, but at a more moderate pace than in

2022. Indeed, gas is now used less to generate electricity than it was
at that time (thanks to the expansion of renewable energy), and to an
even lesser extent in France.
There is a significant risk that energy inflation will spread further to
other components of inflation in the future, and inflation expectations
could eventually reflect this. This is why the European Central Bank
has already raised its key interest rate twice (in June and September)

and, according to our scenario, will likely do so again in December. For
the same reason, we expect the Bank of England to raise its policy rate
in November. Although the cap on energy prices was maintained for
a long time, it has now been raised. As a result, inflation is expected
to rise between now and the end of the year. The aim will then be to
prevent second-round effects.
Inflation can also be a sign of (too much) economic strength. In the

United States, it has exceeded the 2% target since 2021. The main
argument in favour of a new cycle of rate hikes (following the one in
2022-2023) lies in the current resilience of growth and the improving
labour market. The Federal Reserve (Fed) must balance two equally
important objectives: maximum employment and price stability. Howe-
ver, unlike in the 4 th quarter of 2025, when the decline in job crea -
tion justified monetary easing (by 75 basis points), the current sharp

rebound in job creation completely reverses that analysis. According
to our forecasts, the unemployment rate is expected to decrease in
the coming months, which pushes back the prospect of a decline in
core inflation. This situation persists due to both supply constraints
(over which a central bank has no control) and excess demand (which
the Fed can, however, mitigate). Consequently, the Fed raised its key
rate in September and is expected to implement two further rate hikes

between now and January 2027.
In Japan, finally, while headline inflation has been brought down by
government support measures, core inflation remains significant.
Wage pressures are mounting against a backdrop of labour shortages.
The Bank of Japan is therefore being forced to accelerate the pace of its
monetary normalisation.
In all these regions, inflation is currently expected to peak in Q4 2026
or Q1 2027. It is against this backdrop of temporary inflation that rate

hikes could also come to an end by that time (excluding Japan). Howe-
ver, this assumes that once the energy shock—the course of which
remains uncertain—subsides, inflation will fall. The effects of El Niño,
which are still difficult to gauge at present, could take over. Inflation
would then remain above the central banks’ target for a longer period.
Under this scenario, rate hikes would go further than is currently en -
visaged.
THE RISE IN LONG- TERM INTEREST RATES: A FACTOR THAT WILL WEIGH

ON PUBLIC FINANCES
Monetary policies have also to offset the inflationary effects of fiscal
policy, particularly in the United States (where the budget deficit is
high and shows no signs of shrinking) and in Japan (where the new
government is pursuing pro-growth policies). In Europe, fiscal policy
is playing a much smaller role than it did in 2022. While the stimulus
measures implemented at that time did help reduce the inflation rate
in the short term (by nearly 2.5 pp in France, for example), by keeping

demand at a high level, they delayed disinflation and increased the
scale of the monetary tightening required.
The outbreak of the conflict in Iran acted as a catalyst: it triggered a
rise in long-term rates (which had been trending downwards at the
start of the year) to levels close to those of 2006-07. This rise is wides-
pread, but its causes vary. Inflation expectations appear to play a more
significant role in the Eurozone, whereas the rise in real interest rates

seems to be the main factor in the United States and Japan.

EcoPerspectives / 3rd Quarter 2026 / September 2026 economic-research.bnpparibas.com
The bank
for a changing
world
Higher inflation and more resilient real growth than feared point in
the same direction: stronger than expected nominal growth that is li -
kely to be sustained. However, in the long term, high nominal growth
tends to drive up nominal interest rates (as it is a positive sign of more
profitable investment projects), in contrast to the problem of secular

stagnation raised in the 2010s (which combined low nominal growth
with low nominal interest rates).
The other issue is the high level of fiscal deficits (too high for a period
of growth), with various situations. In several countries, the public
debt-to-GDP ratio continues to rise. In the United States, deficit reduc-
tion does not appear to be a priority, to the extent that it is expected
to remain above 6% of GDP at the very least until the end of Donald

Trump’s term of office (with little hope that the mid-term elections will
significantly alter this trend). In the United Kingdom and France, fiscal
consolidation has begun, but it is being hampered by the simultaneous
increase in certain areas of expenditure (debt servicing, defence and
healthcare, due in particular to an ageing population), which is slowing
the pace of consolidation. In Germany, the implementation of invest -
ment plans is leading to a widening of the deficit. While this does not

pose a sustainability problem, it does result in an increase in annual
debt issuance. In Japan, the budget deficit is kept in check by interest
payments that remain low, as the rise in interest rates is recent and
the average maturity of the debt is high (10 years). However, expansio-
nary fiscal policy could pose further problems in terms of debt dyna -
mics if it were to continue through the end of the decade. In Italy and
Spain, fiscal consolidation has been sufficient to stabilise the public

debt-to-GDP ratio (and even to reduce it in Spain), but the high level of
Italy’s debt service it vulnerable to a rise in interest rates.
In all these countries, the fiscal arithmetic is simpler today than it
will be in 2030. Indeed, in most cases, only half of the debt was is -
sued after 2022 (that is, after the end of the zero-interest-rate period).
The debt service has so far only partially risen, except in the United
States, where most of the increase has already occurred due to a shor-

ter average debt maturity (6 years). This means that, for all countries
combined, the rate of GDP growth remains higher than the average
interest rate paid on public debt (or the effective interest rate).
By 2030, the effective rate will have risen and caught up with
GDP growth. As a result, in most cases, the debt service will have re -
turned to the level as a share of GDP seen in the early 2000s. In order to
stabilise public debt-to-GDP ratios, primary balances will need to have

improved. This will even require the generation of primary surpluses,
particularly in Italy (which is already achieving this), France and the
United Kingdom. This is a crucial shift that governments will need to
introduce as early as the 2027 budget; otherwise, they risk postponing
the stabilisation of their public debt-to-GDP ratio once again.
Article completed on 17 September 2026
Stéphane Colliac
stephane.colliac@bnpparibas.com

周四美股 AI 剧烈抛售， CoreWeave 、 Nebius 和 IREN 分别下跌约 10% 、 15% 和 17% 。周四
盘后 IREN 财报超预期， AI Cloud 业务收⼊⼤幅增⻓，叠加周五整体科技股的超跌反弹，美
股早盘 CoreWeave 上涨 9.3% ， IREN 上涨 24.6% ， Nebius 上涨 13.7% 。随后美股午盘，美
媒报道微软不再续租甲⻣⽂部分算⼒，再次打击稍有改善的市场情绪， Neocloud 板块涨幅
收窄。
摘要：
经历前⼀交易⽇的猛烈抛售后， AI 算⼒基础设施股票周五⼀度迎来强劲反弹，但盘中涨势明显降
温。
CoreWeave 由早盘⼀度上涨 9.3% 转为下跌， IREN 涨幅从盘初的 24.6% 收窄⾄约 12% ， Nebius
Group 也从⼀度上涨 13.7% 回落⾄约 2.4% 。截⾄发稿， CoreWeave 周五上涨 4.4% 。
这轮反弹最初受到 IREN 最新财报提振。该公司 AI Cloud 业务收⼊⼤幅增⻓，季度营收超过市场

预期，为近期遭到重挫的 Neocloud 板块提供了积极信号。
不过，财报同时暴露出数据中⼼项⽬交付延期、资本开⽀⾼企等问题，市场对⾏业扩张成本和
盈利兑现能⼒的担忧并未消退。
周四， AI 基础设施股票遭遇⼤幅抛售。美国中期选举结果引发市场对数据中⼼建设监管趋严的担
忧，叠加微软收紧对部分 Neocloud 供应商项⽬的审查， CoreWeave 、 Nebius 和 IREN 分别下跌
约 10% 、 15% 和 17% 。
周五的反弹虽然显⽰部分资⾦开始回补仓位，美股午盘微软继续被曝逐步关闭此前从甲⻣⽂租
⽤的部分计算资源，并决定不再续签相关合同。消息传出后，甲⻣⽂股价迅速跳⽔，此前⼤幅
反弹的 Neocloud 概念股也纷纷回吐涨幅。
IREN 财报超预期，成为板块反弹催化剂
IREN 周四盘后公布的 2027 财年第⼀季度业绩为板块带来⼀定提振。

截⾄ 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇的季度，IREN 实现营收 2.28 亿美元，⾼于分析师预期的 2.21 亿美元。其中， AI
Cloud 业务营收达到 2 亿美元，较上⼀季度的 7100 万美元⼤幅增⻓。公司调整后 EBITDA 为 6600
万美元。
IREN 还披露，其收⼊积压订单达到 203 亿美元，反映出 AI 算⼒需求仍为业务扩张提供⽀撑。
业绩公布后，投资者重新评估 Neocloud 企业的增⻓前景。 IREN 股价盘初⼀度上涨约 24.6% ，
CoreWeave 和 Nebius 也跟随反弹。
不过，强劲的收⼊增⻓并未完全消除市场对 IREN 执⾏能⼒的疑虑。
公司表⽰，由于微软要求调整⽹络设计，以及供应商交付时间安排发⽣变化，部分数据中⼼基
础设施交付出现延期。 IREN 原计划在年底实现超过 40 亿美元的年化收⼊运⾏率⽬标，现在将时
间推迟⾄ 2027 年 1 ⽉，甚⾄可能延后⾄ 2 ⽉底。
与此同时， IREN 预计本财年剩余时间资本开⽀约 150 亿美元。截⾄ 9 ⽉底，公司资本承诺已达到

189.5 亿美元，较三个⽉前的 138.1 亿美元明显增加。
这意味着， AI Cloud 业务的⾼速增⻓仍需要持续投⼊⼤量资⾦，⽽项⽬延期也可能推迟相关收⼊
的兑现时间。
微软连续两天释放收缩信号，不再续租甲⻣⽂部分算⼒
周四，彭博报道称，微软正在加强对 Nebius 、 IREN 和 Nscale 部分数据中⼼项⽬的监督，并退出
其认为⻛险较⾼的项⽬，同时在内部讨论是否继续签署新的 Neocloud 合作协议。
周五美股午盘，彭博援引知情⼈⼠再次报道称，微软近⼏个⽉已开始逐步关闭此前从甲⻣⽂租
⽤的部分计算资源，并决定不再续签相关合同。
报道指出，随着时间推移，微软租⽤的计算资源逐渐⽆法满⾜其不断变化的技术需求。甲⻣⽂
相关服务器搭载的是较早⼀代英伟达芯⽚，其对微软的使⽤价值因此下降。
消息公布后，甲⻣⽂股价迅速由涨转跌，早盘⼀度上涨 3.8% ，随后抹平涨幅并刷新⽇内低点⾄
109.21 美元，盘中跌幅⼀度接近 5.2% 。甲⻣⽂⽅⾯则强调，公司仍然看到市场对 AI 基础设施的
旺盛需求，包括对较早⼀代 GPU 的需求。

这⼀消息也迅速传导⾄ AI 算⼒租赁概念股。 CoreWeave 、 IREN 和 Nebius 均在早盘⼤幅反弹后出
现明显回落。
相关报道进⼀步加剧了市场对第三⽅算⼒供应商商业模式的担忧。
对于 CoreWeave 、 Nebius 和 IREN 等企业⽽⾔，业务扩张⾼度依赖数据中⼼建设、 GPU 采购以
及外部融资。它们需要在项⽬产⽣收⼊之前投⼊⼤量资⾦，因此，电⼒接⼊延误、设备交付推
迟或客⼾调整需求，都可能影响收⼊确认和资⾦回收周期。
融资环境同样构成压⼒。尤其是 CoreWeave ，其⾼杠杆扩张模式已经受到信⽤市场关注。在 AI
基础设施投资回报周期仍存在不确定性的背景下，投资者对资本密集型企业的估值容忍度正在
下降。

Document 281: AI関連企業は巨額の借金を返せるのか ～データセンター投資の急拡大と高まる信用リスク～

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI関連企業は巨額の借金を返せるのか ～データセンター投資の急拡大と高まる信用リスク～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-26
Pages: 6
Status: ok

### Page 1

Research Report

2026 年10 月 26 日

1/6 
AI 関連企業は巨額の借金を返せるのか 
～データセンター投資の急拡大と高まる信用リスク～ 
 資産運用調査部 主席研究員  柏村 祐

（要旨） 
○ 世界的な AI ブームが続くなか、AI 関連企業がデータセンター建設などの巨額投資を賄うた
めに社債発行や借入による資金調達を急増させている。「AI関連企業は手元資金が潤沢で財
務が盤石だ」という従来の常識は揺らぎつつあり、企業の資金不足や信用リスクに目を向け
る必要がある。 
○ AI 投資の拡大は3 つの変化を通じて、企業の財務と金融市場にリスクをもたらしうる。第一
に、テクノロジー企業の社債発行が急増し、借入依存が強まっている。第二に、巨額の設備
投資がAI大手の現金創出力（フリーキャッシュフロー）を圧迫している。第三に、信用力の
低いテクノロジー企業に対する社債市場の警戒が強まり、信用スプレッドが拡大している。 
○ 「AIの成長期待さえあれば巨額の投資もすべて回収できる」と安易に想定すべきではない。
AI 投資の資金を借入などの外部資金に頼る度合いが高まる環境では、収益化の遅れが企業の
信用力低下に直結しやすく、従来の資産配分についても再点検が必要となる。 
○ 個人投資家にとっては、保有する社債ファンドの業種偏りや信用リスクを点検することと、
株式運用において特定の AI 銘柄への過度な集中を避け、安定したキャッシュフローを生む
資産を補完的に組み入れることが、対応策の一つとなるだろう。

1. 「キャッシュリッチなAI企業」の変調 
生成AIの普及を原動力とする世界的なハイテク株高は、日本の個人投資家にとっても資産形成
の大きな追い風となってきた。NISA（少額投資非課税制度）などを通じて米国株式（S&P500な
ど）や全世界株式を保有する投資家の多くは、AI関連企業の成長や株価上昇の恩恵を受けてきた
といえる。もっとも、足元ではAI関連株の値動きが荒くなる場面も増え、巨額の投資が本当に利
益につながるのか不安を感じている投資家も少なくないだろう。一方で、市場には依然として「A
I企業の財務は盤石であり、足元の揺らぎは一時的な調整にすぎない」とみる楽観論も根強い。し
かし、企業の「台所事情」に目を向けると、これまでとは異なるリスクが見えてくる。 「AIブー
ム」と聞くと、華やかな技術革新や株価の上昇ばかりに目が向きがちだが、企業財務の視点に立
つと、巨額のデータセンター投資が企業の手元資金を圧迫し、一部のAI関連企業では借入などの

### Page 2

2/6 
外部資金への依存度が高まりつつある。 
華やかなAI株高の裏側で膨らむ巨額の借金を、AI関連企業は本当に返せるのだろうか。 「テクノ
ロジー企業は潤沢な現金を持ち、財務が健全だ」という従来の常識にとらわれていると、資金不
足や信用リスクの拡大という構造的な変化を見落としかねない。本稿では、AI投資の拡大が引き
起こす 3つの変化として、 「社債発行の急増」 「現金創出力の圧迫」 「信用スプレッドの拡大」を整
理し、個人投資家が資産運用において注意すべき論点を提示したい。なお、本稿では、AI投資を
主導する巨大テクノロジー企業（ハイパースケーラー）から、AI関連事業を手掛ける中堅企業ま
でを幅広く「AI関連企業」と総称する。また、 「テクノロジー企業」は、社債発行統計などにおい
てテクノロジー（IT）部門に分類される企業を指す用語として用いる。

2. AI投資の拡大はなぜ資金不足と信用リスクを招くのか 
(1) AI投資の拡大で急増するテクノロジー企業の社債発行 
現在のAIブームを牽引する巨大テクノロジー企業（ハイパースケーラー）をはじめとするAI関
連企業は、データセンターの建設や先端半導体の確保、電力インフラの整備などに巨額の資金を
投じている。これまでテクノロジー企業は、株式発行や潤沢な手元資金を主な資金源としてきた
が、投資規模の急拡大に伴い、資金調達のあり方が大きく変わりつつある。 
RBA（豪州準備銀行）が2026 年10月に公表した「Financial Stability Review」によれば、AI
関連企業は大規模な投資計画を賄うため、社債やプライベート・クレジット、銀行借入など、負
債による資金調達への依存を強めている。資料1の上段は米国の投資適格社債の発行総額に占め
るテクノロジー部門の割合を、下段はハイパースケーラー（巨大クラウド・AI企業）とテクノロ
ジー部門の実際の社債発行額を示したものである。2026年には、米国の投資適格社債の発行総額
に占めるテクノロジー部門の割合が約27％に達している。また、下段が示すように、足元ではハ
イパースケーラーによる起債が急増し、部門全体の社債発行額を大きく押し上げている。RBAは、
今後計画されているAI関連の設備投資のうち3分の1以上が負債性資金によって賄われるとの市
場の見方を紹介している。つまり、AIブームが続くほど、テクノロジー企業の借入依存が強ま
り、公募・私募の債券市場全体がAI投資の成否に左右されやすくなるという新たな構図が生まれ
ている。

### Page 3

3/6 
資料 1 テクノロジー部門の社債発行が急増、米国投資適格社債の発行総額に占める割合は約 27％に 
（注）米国の非金融企業による投資適格社債の発行額（期間は 2010 年 3 月～2026 年 7 月 17 日）。テクノロジー部門にはコンピ
ュータ・電子機器を含む。上段の割合は 4 四半期移動平均。ハイパースケーラーは Alphabet、Amazon、Meta、Microsoft、
Oracle、NVIDIA を指す。 
（出所）Reserve Bank of Australia, “Financial Stability Review – October 2026”をもとに第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

(2) 巨額の設備投資が企業の現金創出力を圧迫 
なぜ、高い収益力を誇る巨大テクノロジー企業までもが借入への依存を強めているのか。その
背景には、データセンター構築などの設備投資があまりにも巨額であり、本業の稼ぐ力（営業利
益）だけでは投資資金を賄いきれなくなっている現実がある。 
IMF（国際通貨基金）が2026 年10月に公表した「Global Financial Stability Report」は、
AI投資の中心を担う主要ハイパースケーラー5社（Alphabet、Amazon、Meta、Microsoft、
Oracle）の財務状況を分析している。資料2は、これら 5社の設備投資（Capex：Capital 
Expenditure） 、営業利益、およびフリーキャッシュフローの推移を示したものである。5社の設備
投資は 2023年頃から急拡大し、2025年後半から2026年にかけて営業利益を上回る規模に達して
いる。その結果、フリーキャッシュフロー（右軸）は急減し、2023～24年のピーク時には四半期
ベースで600 億米ドルを超えていたものが、2026年にはほぼゼロまで落ち込んでいる。もっと
も、ハイパースケーラー各社は現時点では強固なバランスシートと十分な流動性を維持してい
る。しかし、激しい競争圧力や技術の陳腐化に対応するため、企業が投資の手を緩めることは難
しい。今後、AIの収益化が市場の期待を下回るようなことがあれば、企業の現金創出力は一段と
圧迫され、追加の資金調達や財務負担の増大を招く可能性がある。

### Page 4

4/6

資料 2 主要ハイパースケーラー5 社の設備投資が急増し、2026 年のフリーキャッシュフローはほぼゼロに 
（注）主要ハイパースケーラー5 社（Alphabet、Amazon、Meta、Microsoft、Oracle）の設備投資（Capex、左軸）、営業利益（左軸）、
およびフリーキャッシュフロー（右軸）の四半期ごとの推移（単位：10 億米ドル）。 
（出所）IMF, “Global Financial Stability Report, October 2026”をもとに第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

(3) 信用力の低いテクノロジー企業に対する社債市場の警戒 
さらに、AI投資への借入依存が高まるなか、社債市場ではテクノロジー企業の信用リスクに対
する警戒が強まりつつある。株式市場ではAIの成長期待が注目されやすいが、元利金の確実な返
済を重視する債券市場では、企業の財務体力やビジネスモデルの持続可能性に対する選別がシビ
アに進んでいる。 
ECB（欧州中央銀行）が2026 年5月に公表した「Financial Stability Review」では、AIを巡
る懸念が株式市場だけでなく社債市場にも波及していると指摘している。資料3 は、2025年 3月
から 2026年5 月にかけてのユーロ圏および米国におけるハイイールド（HY、低格付け）社債の信
用スプレッドの推移を示したものである。米国・ユーロ圏ともに、ハイイールド債市場全体（米
国HY、ユーロ圏HY）のスプレッドが300ベーシスポイント（bp）前後で比較的落ち着いて推移し
ていたのに対し、信用力の低いテクノロジー企業（米国HYテック、ユーロ圏HYテック）のスプレ
ッドは 2026年に入り500bp超へと大きく拡大し、同年 5月時点でも450bp前後と、市場全体を上
回る水準で推移していた。 
ECBは、AIが既存のソフトウェア企業などのビジネスモデルを脅かすリスクに加え、内部資金だ
けでなく借入に頼ってAI投資を拡大させる動きが金融安定上の懸念になりうると指摘している。
巨額のAI投資ブームの裏側では、信用力の低いテクノロジー企業に対する市場の警戒が強まり、
企業の借入依存が信用市場にも新たなリスクをもたらしつつある。 「借りた巨額の資金を本当に返

### Page 5

5/6 
せるのか」という市場の問いかけがすでに始まっているのである。

資料 3 信用力の低いテクノロジー企業の信用スプレッドはハイイールド債市場全体より大きく拡大 
（注）2025 年 3 月 1 日～2026 年 5 月 19 日におけるユーロ圏および米国のハイイールド（HY）社債の信用スプレッド（OIS カーブ 
に対する上乗せ金利、時価総額加重平均、単位：ベーシスポイント）。「HY テック」はハイイールド格付けのテクノロジー企業
を指す。 
縦の破線は左から 2025 年 4 月の米国の関税発表、前回 FSR（金融安定報告書）公表時点（2025 年 11 月）、中東戦争の開 
始（2026 年 3 月初旬）を示す。 
（出所）European Central Bank, “Financial Stability Review, May 2026”をもとに第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

3. 「AI借金時代」の資産防衛術 
第 2 章で見てきたように、AI ブームの裏側では「社債発行の急増」 「現金創出力の圧迫」 「信用
スプレッドの拡大」という3 つの変化が同時に進行している。この視点に立てば、AIブームが抱
える死角は「株価の割高感」だけでは説明できない。巨大テクノロジー企業から中堅のテクノロ
ジー企業に至るまで、AI投資の拡大に伴い、一部の企業では自己資金だけでなく、借入などの外
部資金への依存度が高まりつつある。 
ここで重要なのは、 「テクノロジー企業はキャッシュリッチで財務が盤石だ」と安易に考えない
ことである。仮にAI の収益化が遅れれば、膨らんだ借入負担が企業の業績や信用力を直撃するシ
ナリオも視野に入れる必要がある。こうした「AI借金時代」を迎えるなか、個人投資家には、従
来のポートフォリオに潜む信用リスクを改めて点検することが求められる。

### Page 6

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
6/6 
第一に、社債ファンドやハイイールド債（低格付け社債）における「信用リスク」を過小評価
しないことである。2026年の米国の投資適格社債の発行総額に占めるテクノロジー部門の割合が
約27％に達し、信用力の低いテクノロジー企業のスプレッドが拡大している現状では、 「債券だか
ら安心」 「株安を社債がカバーしてくれる」という従来の前提が働きにくい可能性がある。AI の収
益期待が後退する局面では、AI 関連株の下落と信用スプレッドの拡大（社債価格の下落）が同時
に進みかねない点に注意が必要だ。債券を保有する場合には、表面的な利回りの高さだけでハイ
イールド債や社債ファンドを選好するのではなく、組入資産の業種の偏りを確認することや、信
用リスクの低い先進国国債などへの分散を検討することも、選択肢の一つとなる。 
第二に、株式運用において「AI関連だから買う」という一括りの発想から脱却し、企業の現金
創出力と業種分散を重視することである。NISAなどを活用してS&P500や全世界株式連動商品を保
有する投資家は多いが、これらの指数では、米国の巨大テクノロジー企業の構成比率が高まって
おり、特にS&P500では一部の企業への集中が進んでいる。指数連動商品をポートフォリオの中核
に据えつつも、補完的な資産として、安定したフリーキャッシュフローを生み出す高配当バリュ
ー株やディフェンシブ株、あるいはAIデータセンター投資の受け皿となる電力・インフラ関連株
などを組み入れることは、AI 企業の資金不足や淘汰の波に備える選択肢の一つとなるだろう。た
だし、特定のテーマや業種に偏りすぎないよう、投資比率には十分な注意が必要である。 
AI 革命は株式市場に大きな成長機会をもたらす一方で、企業の財務構造や信用市場のリスクを
も変えつつある。これからは、 「AIだから成長する」という発想にとどまらず、その裏側で膨らむ
「借金の行方」まで見据えることが重要になる。AI 企業のフリーキャッシュフローや信用スプレ
ッドの動きも、株価と同様に注視する。それが、巨額のAI投資時代において投資家に求められる
新たな視点であろう。

以 上

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: managing expectations
By Ryan Wells, Illiana Jain & Elliot Clarke
11:04 October 23 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
With little domestic data of significance, participants’ attention focused on RBA Deputy Governor
Hauser's fireside chat, which saw an abrupt shift in market expectations of a follow-up rate hike at the
November meeting.
Hauser warned in his remarks that the current inflation outlook was "materially worse" than the RBA's

### Page 2

August forecasts. Three upside inflation risks were characterised as having "crystallised" ahead of the
September meeting – the Middle East, the AI boom, and domestic supply constraints. Hauser noted the
Middle East picture "looks worse" given oil prices are "about a third higher than [the RBA] assumed in
August”. He then positioned AI and data centre developments among an "extraordinary sequence of
positive demand shocks around the world", with "higher AI prices, tech prices feeding through into

CPI". Finally, on domestic capacity pressures, the Deputy Governor noted that "inflation's been coming
in at or a little bit above expectations, despite an apparent easing in the labour market". He went on to
state that the staff are currently assessing "whether [the RBA] need to change [its] judgement on
capacity pressures in the labour market again" – in other words, revise up their assumption for the
NAIRU.
Hauser's comments prompted a material reassessment of market pricing for a follow-up November

hike, from just a 20% chance at the end of last week to a 50/50 proposition today ahead of next week's
Q3 CPI. For that release, we are forecasting a 1.3% (4.1%yr) lift in headline inflation and a 0.9 (3.5%yr)
increase in trimmed mean inflation. Our preview provides more detail behind the component mix for Q3
as well as our upward revisions to the preliminary Q4 forecast. Provided inflation prints broadly in line
with our expectations, the RBA is likely to hike by 25bp at the November meeting.

Across in New Zealand, consumer prices rose 0.7% in Q3 and annual inflation eased from 4.1%yr to
3.7%yr. Under the surface of this ‘as expected’ read though, several closely watched measures of core
inflation took a step higher to now be at or slightly above the top of the RBNZ’s target band. Our NZ
economics team consequently expect the RBNZ will raise the OCR 25bps to 3.00% at its October
policy review, while retaining a 4.00% terminal OCR forecast for September 2027.

Earlier in the week, China's economy surprised to the upside in Q3, GDP growth accelerating from
0.9%qtr to 1.2%qtr, lifting annual growth from 4.3%yr to 4.5%yr – keeping authorities on track to
achieve their 4.5-5.0% full-year growth objective.
While annual retail sales growth improved in September, rising from 0.4%yr to 1.1%yr, the current pace
of growth is only a quarter of the gain seen just a year ago and very weak versus history. Consumer
spending continues to be constrained by weak confidence, particularly with respect to employment

prospects and wealth. On housing wealth, five years into the property downturn, house prices continue
to decline month-on-month and investment remains in a deep downturn, -20.6%ytd.
Total fixed asset investment has been detracting from annual GDP growth since April owing to a lull in
high-tech investment, a lack of confidence in future demand amongst domestically oriented businesses
as well as property’s malaise. For aggregate growth to hold up through 2027 and 2028 as the

contribution from net exports moderates, broad-based stimulus in scale will prove necessary. Expect
recent targeted support measures to be broadened and given much greater scale in coming months.
Finally, in the UK, inflation accelerated from 3.1%yr to 3.5%yr in September, exceeding both market
expectations and the Bank of England's July forecast. The surprise was largely driven by energy, fuel
prices rising 5.4%mth. However, core inflation also edged up from 2.6%yr to 2.8%yr as services

inflation increased from 3.4%yr to 3.7%yr. While not an immediate concern inflation, the labour market
showing resilience, the ILO unemployment rate falling from 4.9% to 4.7% in August, suggests demand

### Page 3

Related articles
risks for inflation will need close monitoring as well as supply-side concerns.
Next week will see the focus once again return to the US as the FOMC meet and US Q3 GDP is
released. For both events, the implications for risks to the outlook will be the focus. The week after, the
US mid-term elections are due.

The post-midterm dollar correction requires a worsening of USD
rate diﬀerentials to turn into a downturn. But as oil prices fall,
other central banks are facing similar or even larger dovish
repricing than the Fed. Data is key now, but indications are that
they won’t turn USD-negative soon. Today, expect a hike in the
UK and rate holds in Norway and Mexico

Francesco Pesole, Frantisek Taborsky and Chris Turner

USD: Front-end rates should prevent a

The Democratic sweep at the midterms delivered the negative
dollar impact we had expected. But as we argued before the vote,
we didn’t see a case for a bigger USD downturn. In our view, rates
need to start moving against the dollar to justify a greater
adjustment, unless idiosyncratic US asset underperformance fuels
the move. But fears that a Democratic sweep would hit tech stocks
didn’t materialise. Instead, a potential relief that the midterm risk

The key hurdle for a bigger USD downturn is that lower oil prices are
not unequivocally USD-negative. Let’s look at yesterday’s moves in
rates. As crude dropped 8%, two-year USD swap rates fell 5bp,
broadly in line with CAD, AUD and SEK swaps. EUR, GBP and NZD
2yr swap rates, however, fell more, by around 8-10bp. To us, that is
the most compelling argument against chasing the dollar much

lower. Poorer US data and/or a more dovish Fed remain the
necessary conditions for front-end USD rates to fall more than
peers, and by extension for a broader USD downturn.
Instead, data has remained broadly supportive of Fed tightening
expectations. Yesterday, the ISM services index was a tad softer
than expected but remains well into expansionary territory. The
employment subindex rose to 51.4, the highest since February.
Paired with strong ADP payrolls at 86k, that’s likely to reinforce

expectations for a robust payroll print tomorrow – consensus is 65k,
but the whisper number has risen to 70k.
Today’s data highlights are the Challenger jobs cut ﬁgures for
October and 3Q unit labour costs. We favour a stabilisation around
101.0-101.50 in DXY in the coming days, rather than another break
lower.
Francesco Pesole
Elsewhere, Banxico announces its rate decision later today. The
peso has been the worst-performing EM currency over the last two

months after Banxico said it would not be following the Fed with a
rate hike in September. In fact, some are looking for Banxico to heap
more fuel on the peso’s ﬁre with a rate cut today. Such a move
could see USD/MXN push to 19. Either way, Banxico has slashed the
peso’s rate protection to ultra-low levels, and the peso remains very
vulnerable to global shocks.
Chris Turner

Klaas Knot delivered a few remarks yesterday after the Netherlands

ﬁrmly hawkish, claiming second-round inﬂation eﬀects will

EUR-positive event given his hawkish views, in our view.
Meanwhile, eurozone inﬂation accelerated from 3.8% to 4.0% as
expected in October. That brings the ECB closer to a December hike,
as argued here by our macro team, even if core inﬂation remained
anchored at 2.5%.

gap widened against the euro yesterday as oil prices tumbled. A
similar dynamic prevented EUR/USD from rallying in the summer

when oil fell back to $70. Now, at least one more ECB hike – likely in
December – looks like a done deal, providing a stronger ﬂoor for
eurozone rate expectations. Still, this rate component, combined
with lingering French risk, leaves us unexcited about a break higher
in EUR/USD. We continue to see little upside potential beyond
1.130, our year-end target.
Francesco Pesole
GBP: BoE to resume tightening today
We expect the Bank of England to hike rates today by 25bp to 4.0%

(full preview here), in line with market expectations and consensus.
A roughly 25% rise in household energy bills in January now looks
increasingly likely, and we expect headline inﬂation to peak just
above 4% early next year. That has often been indicated as the
pain threshold for the BoE. Still, evidence of second-round
inﬂationary eﬀects remains limited.
We expect a 7-2 vote for a hike, and don’t see the BoE committing
to further tightening at this stage. A follow-up move in February

remains our base case, but policymakers should keep the
emphasis ﬁrmly on incoming evidence of energy pass-through into

NOK: Norges Bank on hold today
Norges Bank should keep rates on hold today. Having been one of
the quickest central banks to respond to fresh inﬂationary risks and

remain for some time, but we do not expect any further tightening
in Norway, in line with Norges Bank’s September rate projections.
Markets are pricing in just under 50% chances of another hike by

March.
The krone is facing some headwinds due to the big drop in oil
prices. However, it’s protected by much less hawkish pricing than
other central banks, therefore facing less dovish pressure on energy
price corrections. Yesterday, the NOK two-year swap rate dropped
only 2bp. While this oil move can take EUR/NOK back to 10.50, a
return to $80-85/bll may be needed for a return to the late
September 10.90 levels. We see the pair ending the year in the

10.55-10.65 area.

CEE: Lower energy prices overshadow

rates, and wages, alongside a hawkish external environment, while
the GDP growth forecast will likely be revised downward. The CNB

has scaled back its forward guidance. We do not expect ﬁrm
commitments regarding future policy steps today, but rather an

Document 359: Gridlock Won & Markets Cheer

Source PDF: all report pdf/Gridlock Won & Markets Cheer.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-05
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: Gridlock Won & Markets Cheer
Date:    6 hours ago
Gridlock Won & Markets Cheer

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
Gridlock Won & Markets Cheer
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 4 Nov 2026
View in browser

I. Why Wall Street Is Cheering the Blue Sweep
Ahead of Tuesday's midterm elections, we warned that a Democratic
sweep of Congress could deliver a cold shower to Wall Street. It still could,
but it didn't do so today. Democrats captured both chambers of Congress,
flipping seven Republican-held Senate seats, including historic victories in
Texas, Kansas, and South Carolina. Financial markets put a positive spin
on the blue wave.
Oil prices fell on hopes that the US and Iran are more likely now to resolve
their war diplomatically. If so, then the outlook for inflation will improve. So
the Fed might not have to raise the federal funds rate as much. The

### Page 2

federal deficit might not widen further if Democrats refuse to increase
defense spending, while President Donald Trump vetoes their attempts to
provide Medicare for all. If Democrats slow the AI buildout, hyperscalers'
profits and free cash flow will increase. Fewer AI-related bond issues
would also help keep a lid on bond yields.
Initially, Nasdaq-100 futures plunged as much as 2.4% overnight, while
S&P 500 futures fell 1.4%. But the selloff reversed after Trump struck a

surprisingly conciliatory tone, congratulating Democrats and saying he
was willing to work with them.
That is when the cheer on Wall Street started. The Dow surged 3.0%
today, the strongest post-midterm rally since 1982. The S&P 500 jumped
1.6%, its best session in three months. The Nasdaq-100 advanced 0.9%.
The relief rally extended beyond equities. The 10-year Treasury yield fell
to 4.90%, back in what we have called the "old normal" range of
4.00%-5.00% (chart).

The dollar index (DXY) fell more than 1.5%, its biggest decline since
September. Gold rallied back above $4,000 an ounce. It might be finally
bottoming.

### Page 3

de-escalation in the Middle East
Brent crude plunged 8.7% to $94.62 a barrel amid hopes of Middle East
de-escalation. The nearby futures price of gasoline plunged to $2.73
(chart).
Investors may be warming up to the idea that some constraints on the AI
buildout could be healthy, as we have been arguing recently. Democratic
victories in key data center states, including Texas and Georgia, raise the
prospect of tighter construction restrictions amid concerns about electricity

and water consumption.
Hyperscalers rallied today, with Amazon and Microsoft gaining more than
6%, as capital discipline could ease concerns about AI investment returns
and improve free cash flow (chart). AI infrastructure providers struggled,

### Page 4

however. The PHLX Semiconductor Index fell 3.1% amid fears of weaker
demand from a slower buildout. We may be entering an AI Goldilocks
zone, with enough restraint to take some air out of the bubble without
derailing the boom.
II. The US Economy Continues To Perform Well
The economy and, therefore, earnings remain fundamentally resilient,
which continues to support the bull market. With the midterms behind us
and political uncertainty receding, investors can refocus on these strong

fundamentals. Here are the highlights of today's upbeat economic
indicators:
(1) ISM Services PMI. The ISM-NM PMI edged down modestly to 54.5 in
October, marking its 28th consecutive month of expansion (chart).
Underlying demand remained solid, with new orders and business activity
both comfortably above the 50.0 expansion threshold. Encouragingly, the
employment index rose from 50.1 to 51.4, marking its second consecutive
month of expansion.

### Page 5

Inflation is still a concern. The ISM prices-paid indexes remained elevated
in October (chart).
(2) Labor market. The average of the ISM employment indexes rose to
51.9 in October, its highest reading in roughly three years (chart). ADP
reported an 86,000 increase in private payrolls in October, beating
consensus expectations of 67,000.

### Page 6

(3) Trade balance. The latest trade data point to resilient US and global
economic growth. Merchandise imports rose 2.4% m/m in September to a
record $3.44 trillion annualized, while exports increased 2.5% m/m to
$2.09 trillion. The merchandise trade deficit widened to $1.35 trillion
annualized (chart).
Nominal and real US merchandise exports plus imports rose 21.1% y/y
and 10.1%, respectively, in September (chart). That suggests global trade
remains firmly in expansion territory and is consistent with strong global

economic growth.

### Page 7

Selected technology imports reached a record $61.3 billion in September,
reflecting the AI buildout’s heavy reliance on imported semiconductors,
servers, and other hardware (chart).

TAKING STOCK

The Reserve Bank of India (RBI) has adopted a 
multi-layered defence strategy to support the 
rupee this year.

This has entailed a combination of outright 
intervention in spot/ forwards, regulatory 
action, liquidity steps, administrative measures 
and moves to curb import -related dollar 
demand.

The rupee has appreciated by 1.8% against the 
dollar in the past five days since recent 
measures took effect, paring YTD depreciation 
to 5.5% . High oil prices, weakness in capital 
inflows, a firm dollar , tariffs/ geopolitical 
worries, energy , and risk -off sentiments have 
kept the INR under pressure since last year.
